-- 11. Prism Field 3D: map-wide floating prisms with layered spinning cores.
AddWorldEffect("Prism Field 3D", function(folder)
    local list = {}
    local origin = (LocalPlayer.Character and PortalGetRoot(LocalPlayer.Character) and PortalGetRoot(LocalPlayer.Character).Position) or Vector3.zero
    for i = 1, 12 do
        local p = GroundPoint(origin, Vector2.new(math.random(-180, 180), math.random(-180, 180)), 120)
        local height = 3.5 + math.random() * 6
        local prism = Make3DPart(folder, Vector3.new(0.9, 2.6, 0.9), Color3.fromRGB(225, 65, 82))
        prism.CFrame = CFrame.new(p + Vector3.new(0, height, 0)) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(12), math.random() * math.pi, math.rad(-10))
        local mesh = Instance.new("SpecialMesh")
        mesh.MeshType = Enum.MeshType.Wedge
        mesh.Scale = Vector3.new(0.7, 1.8, 0.7)
        mesh.Parent = prism

        local core = Make3DPart(folder, Vector3.new(0.32, 0.32, 0.32), Color3.fromRGB(255, 155, 165))
        core.Shape = Enum.PartType.Ball
        core.Position = prism.Position + Vector3.new(0, 1.65, 0)

        local light = Instance.new("PointLight")
        light.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 70, 85)
        light.Range = 10
        light.Brightness = 1.3
        light.Shadows = false
        light.Parent = core

        table.insert(list, {
            prism = prism,
            core = core,
            light = light,
            base = prism.CFrame,
            coreBase = core.Position,
            phase = math.random() * 6.28,
            spin = 0.55 + math.random() * 0.75,
            height = height
        })
    end
    return list
end, function(list, dt, t)
    for _, item in ipairs(list) do
        if item.prism and item.prism.Parent then
            local bob = math.sin(t * 1.4 + item.phase) * 0.35
            item.prism.CFrame = item.base * CFrame.new(0, bob, 0) * CFrame.Angles(0, t * item.spin, math.sin(t * 0.9 + item.phase) * 0.08)
            item.prism.Color = Color3.fromHSV((0.98 + math.sin(t * 0.18 + item.phase) * 0.025) % 1, 0.78, 1)
            item.core.Position = item.coreBase + Vector3.new(0, bob + math.sin(t * 2.1 + item.phase) * 0.16, 0)
            local pulse = (math.sin(t * 2.4 + item.phase) + 1) * 0.5
            item.core.Size = Vector3.new(0.24 + pulse * 0.22, 0.24 + pulse * 0.22, 0.24 + pulse * 0.22)
            item.light.Brightness = 0.7 + pulse * 1.8
        end
    end
end, 0.033)

-- 12. Orbital Relays 3D: stationary world relays with three orbiting satellites.
AddWorldEffect("Orbital Relays 3D", function(folder)
    local list = {}
    local origin = (LocalPlayer.Character and PortalGetRoot(LocalPlayer.Character) and PortalGetRoot(LocalPlayer.Character).Position) or Vector3.zero
    for i = 1, 8 do
        local p = GroundPoint(origin, Vector2.new(math.random(-210, 210), math.random(-210, 210)), 120)
        local base = Make3DPart(folder, Vector3.new(1.1, 3.2, 1.1), Color3.fromRGB(52, 45, 55), Enum.Material.Metal)
        base.Position = p + Vector3.new(0, 1.6, 0)
        local core = Make3DPart(folder, Vector3.new(0.62, 0.62, 0.62), Color3.fromRGB(255, 65, 82))
        core.Shape = Enum.PartType.Ball
        core.Position = p + Vector3.new(0, 3.35, 0)
        local halo = Make3DPart(folder, Vector3.new(2.2, 0.10, 2.2), Color3.fromRGB(255, 90, 105))
        halo.Shape = Enum.PartType.Cylinder
        halo.CFrame = CFrame.new(p + Vector3.new(0, 3.35, 0)) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.rad(90))
        halo.Transparency = 0.28

        local satellites = {}
        for j = 1, 3 do
            local sat = Make3DPart(folder, Vector3.new(0.26, 0.26, 0.26), Color3.fromRGB(255, 165, 175))
            sat.Shape = Enum.PartType.Ball
            sat.Position = core.Position
            table.insert(satellites, sat)
        end

        local light = Instance.new("PointLight")
        light.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 45, 65)
        light.Range = 12
        light.Brightness = 1.1
        light.Shadows = false
        light.Parent = core

        table.insert(list, {
            pos = p,
            base = base,
            core = core,
            halo = halo,
            satellites = satellites,
            light = light,
            phase = math.random() * 6.28,
            radius = 2.4 + math.random() * 0.45
        })
    end
    return list
end, function(list, dt, t)
    for _, item in ipairs(list) do
        if item.core and item.core.Parent then
            local center = item.pos + Vector3.new(0, 3.35 + math.sin(t * 1.25 + item.phase) * 0.16, 0)
            item.core.Position = center
            item.halo.CFrame = CFrame.new(center) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(90), t * 0.85 + item.phase, 0)
            item.halo.Transparency = 0.20 + (math.sin(t * 2 + item.phase) + 1) * 0.10
            for j, sat in ipairs(item.satellites) do
                local a = t * (0.85 + j * 0.12) + item.phase + (j - 1) * (math.pi * 2 / 3)
                local y = math.sin(t * 1.5 + item.phase + j) * 0.35
                sat.Position = center + Vector3.new(math.cos(a) * item.radius, y, math.sin(a) * item.radius)
            end
            item.light.Brightness = 0.65 + (math.sin(t * 2.6 + item.phase) + 1) * 0.8
        end
    end
end, 0.033)

-- 13. Wave Columns 3D: map-wide vertical energy columns with travelling rings.
AddWorldEffect("Wave Columns 3D", function(folder)
    local list = {}
    local origin = (LocalPlayer.Character and PortalGetRoot(LocalPlayer.Character) and PortalGetRoot(LocalPlayer.Character).Position) or Vector3.zero
    for i = 1, 9 do
        local p = GroundPoint(origin, Vector2.new(math.random(-240, 240), math.random(-240, 240)), 140)
        local height = 7 + math.random() * 5
        local column = Make3DPart(folder, Vector3.new(0.34, height, 0.34), Color3.fromRGB(245, 80, 95))
        column.Transparency = 0.24
        column.Position = p + Vector3.new(0, height * 0.5, 0)

        local rings = {}
        for j = 1, 3 do
            local ring = Make3DPart(folder, Vector3.new(2.0 + j * 0.25, 0.08, 2.0 + j * 0.25), Color3.fromRGB(255, 115, 125))
            ring.Shape = Enum.PartType.Cylinder
            ring.Transparency = 0.28
            ring.CFrame = CFrame.new(p + Vector3.new(0, 1.0 + j * 1.8, 0)) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.rad(90))
            table.insert(rings, ring)
        end

        local light = Instance.new("PointLight")
        light.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 55, 70)
        light.Range = 11
        light.Brightness = 1.0
        light.Shadows = false
        light.Parent = column

        table.insert(list, {
            base = p,
            column = column,
            rings = rings,
            light = light,
            phase = math.random() * 6.28,
            height = height
        })
    end
    return list
end, function(list, dt, t)
    for _, item in ipairs(list) do
        if item.column and item.column.Parent then
            local pulse = (math.sin(t * 1.8 + item.phase) + 1) * 0.5
            item.column.Transparency = 0.38 - pulse * 0.24
            item.column.Size = Vector3.new(0.26 + pulse * 0.14, item.height * (0.92 + pulse * 0.08), 0.26 + pulse * 0.14)
            for j, ring in ipairs(item.rings) do
                local progress = (t * 0.42 + item.phase * 0.08 + j * 0.31) % 1
                local y = 0.8 + progress * (item.height - 0.3)
                local size = 1.2 + progress * 4.8
                ring.Size = Vector3.new(0.08, size, size)
                ring.CFrame = CFrame.new(item.base + Vector3.new(0, y, 0)) * CFrame.Angles(0, t * (0.55 + j * 0.12), math.rad(90))
                ring.Transparency = 0.16 + progress * 0.62
            end
            item.light.Brightness = 0.55 + pulse * 1.4
        end
    end
end, 0.033)

    "ScreenGui"
)

ScreenGui.Name =
    "YukiShaderEngine"

ScreenGui.ResetOnSpawn =
    false

ScreenGui.IgnoreGuiInset =
    true

ScreenGui.DisplayOrder =
    999999

ScreenGui.ZIndexBehavior =
    Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling

pcall(function()
    if syn and syn.protect_gui then
        syn.protect_gui(ScreenGui)
    end
end)

ScreenGui.Parent =
    CoreGui

--============================================================
-- UI SOUNDS
--============================================================
local UISFX = {
    Click = Instance.new("Sound"),
    Hover = Instance.new("Sound")
}

UISFX.Click.Name = "YukiUI_Click"
UISFX.Click.SoundId = "rbxassetid://113397864512278"
UISFX.Click.Volume = 0.42
UISFX.Click.PlaybackSpeed = 1
UISFX.Click.Parent = SoundService

UISFX.Hover.Name = "YukiUI_Hover"
UISFX.Hover.SoundId = "rbxassetid://18856494234"
UISFX.Hover.Volume = 0.18
UISFX.Hover.PlaybackSpeed = 1.04
UISFX.Hover.Parent = SoundService

local function PlayUISFX(kind)
    local sound = UISFX[kind]
    if sound then
        sound.TimePosition = 0
        sound:Play()
    end
end

local function BindUISFX(button)
    if not button or not button:IsA("TextButton") then
        return
    end
    if button:GetAttribute("YukiSFXBound") then
        return
    end
    button:SetAttribute("YukiSFXBound", true)
    button.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
        PlayUISFX("Hover")
    end)
    button.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        PlayUISFX("Click")
    end)
end

ScreenGui.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)
    if obj:IsA("TextButton") then
        BindUISFX(obj)
    end
end)

--============================================================
-- EFFECT FACTORY
--============================================================

function _Yuki_CreateEffect(
    effectType,
    properties
)
    local effect

    if effectType == "Bloom" then

        effect =
            Instance.new(
                "BloomEffect"
            )

    elseif effectType == "ColorCorrection" then

        effect =
            Instance.new(
                "ColorCorrectionEffect"
            )

    elseif effectType == "Blur" then

        effect =
            Instance.new(
                "BlurEffect"
            )

    elseif effectType == "DepthOfField" then

        effect =
            Instance.new(
                "DepthOfFieldEffect"
            )

    elseif effectType == "SunRays" then

        effect =
            Instance.new(
                "SunRaysEffect"
            )

    elseif effectType == "Atmosphere" then

        effect =
            Instance.new(
                "Atmosphere"
            )

    else
        return nil
    end

    effect.Name =
        CONFIG.ShaderPrefix
        .. effectType

    for property, value in pairs(
        properties or {}
    ) do

        pcall(function()
            effect[property] =
                value
        end)
    end

    effect.Parent =
        Lighting

    Effects[effectType] =
        effect

    return effect
end

--============================================================
-- DESTROY OUR EFFECTS
--============================================================

function _Yuki_DestroyShaderEffects()
    for key, effect in pairs(
        Effects
    ) do

        if effect then
            SafeDestroy(effect)
        end

        Effects[key] = nil
    end

    -- safety cleanup
    for _, child in ipairs(
        Lighting:GetChildren()
    ) do

        if child.Name:sub(
            1,
            #CONFIG.ShaderPrefix
        ) == CONFIG.ShaderPrefix then

            SafeDestroy(child)
        end
    end

    if MotionBlurEffect then
        SafeDestroy(
            MotionBlurEffect
        )
        MotionBlurEffect = nil
    end
end

--============================================================
-- VIGNETTE
--============================================================

function _Yuki_DestroyVignette()
    if VignetteGui then
        SafeDestroy(
            VignetteGui
        )

        VignetteGui = nil
    end
end

function _Yuki_CreateVignette(
    strength
)
    _Yuki_DestroyVignette()

    VignetteGui =
        Instance.new(
            "ScreenGui"
        )

    VignetteGui.Name =
        CONFIG.VignetteName

    VignetteGui.IgnoreGuiInset =
        true

    VignetteGui.ResetOnSpawn =
        false

    VignetteGui.DisplayOrder =
        999998

    pcall(function()
        if syn and syn.protect_gui then
            syn.protect_gui(
                VignetteGui
            )
        end
    end)

    VignetteGui.Parent =
        CoreGui

    local top =
        Instance.new("Frame")

    top.Size =
        UDim2.new(
            1,
            0,
            0.27,
            0
        )

    top.BackgroundColor3 =
        Color3.new(
            0,
            0,
            0
        )

    top.BackgroundTransparency =
        Clamp(
            1 - strength * 0.72,
            0.18,
            0.98
        )

    top.BorderSizePixel =
        0

    top.Parent =
        VignetteGui

    local topGradient =
        Instance.new(
            "UIGradient"
        )

    topGradient.Rotation =
        90

    topGradient.Transparency =
        NumberSequence.new({
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(
                0,
                0
            ),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(
                1,
                1
            )
        })

    topGradient.Parent =
        top

    local bottom =
        top:Clone()

    bottom.AnchorPoint =
        Vector2.new(
            0,
            1
        )

    bottom.Position =
        UDim2.fromScale(
            0,
            1
        )

    bottom.Rotation =
        180

    bottom.Parent =
        VignetteGui

    local left =
        Instance.new("Frame")

    left.Size =
        UDim2.new(
            0.20,
            0,
            1,
            0
        )

    left.BackgroundColor3 =
        Color3.new(
            0,
            0,
            0
        )

    left.BackgroundTransparency =
        Clamp(
            1 - strength * 0.68,
            0.18,
            0.98
        )

    left.BorderSizePixel =
        0

    left.Parent =
        VignetteGui

    local leftGradient =
        Instance.new(
            "UIGradient"
        )

    leftGradient.Transparency =
        NumberSequence.new({
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(
                0,
                0
            ),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(
                1,
                1
            )
        })

    leftGradient.Parent =
        left

    local right =
        left:Clone()

    right.AnchorPoint =
        Vector2.new(
            1,
            0
        )

    right.Position =
        UDim2.fromScale(
            1,
            0
        )

    right.Rotation =
        180

    right.Parent =
        VignetteGui
end

function _Yuki_UpdateVignette()
    if not ShaderState.Vignette then
        _Yuki_DestroyVignette()
        return
    end

    _Yuki_CreateVignette(
        ShaderState.VignetteStrength
    )
end

--============================================================
-- MOTION BLUR
--============================================================

function _Yuki_StopMotionBlur()
    if MotionBlurEffect then
        SafeDestroy(
            MotionBlurEffect
        )
        MotionBlurEffect = nil
    end

    Effects.MotionBlur = nil
    LastCameraCFrame = nil
end

function _Yuki_StartMotionBlur()
    if MotionBlurEffect then
        return
    end

    MotionBlurEffect =
        Instance.new(
            "BlurEffect"
        )

    MotionBlurEffect.Name =
        CONFIG.ShaderPrefix
        .. "MotionBlur"

    MotionBlurEffect.Size =
        0

    MotionBlurEffect.Parent =
        Lighting

    Effects.MotionBlur =
        MotionBlurEffect

    local camera =
        workspace.CurrentCamera

    if camera then
        LastCameraCFrame =
            camera.CFrame
    end
end

--============================================================
-- Kill Atmosphere
--============================================================
function _Yuki_ApplyKillAtmosphere(enable)
    if enable then
        local atm = nil

        for _, child in ipairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do
            if child:IsA("Atmosphere")
                and child.Name:sub(1, #CONFIG.ShaderPrefix) ~= CONFIG.ShaderPrefix then

                atm = child
                break
            end
        end

        if atm then
            UISettings.SavedAtmosphere = atm
            atm.Parent = nil
            UISettings.AtmosphereRemoved = true
        else
            UISettings.SavedAtmosphere = nil
            UISettings.AtmosphereRemoved = false
        end
    else
        if UISettings.SavedAtmosphere
            and UISettings.AtmosphereRemoved then

            pcall(function()
                UISettings.SavedAtmosphere.Parent = Lighting
            end)

            UISettings.SavedAtmosphere = nil
            UISettings.AtmosphereRemoved = false
        end
    end
end

-- Restores an Atmosphere hidden by Kill Atmosphere.
-- This is intentionally done before preset cleanup so the object is not
-- left detached when changing presets or unloading the engine.
function _Yuki_RestoreKilledAtmosphere()
    if UISettings.SavedAtmosphere
        and UISettings.AtmosphereRemoved then

        pcall(function()
            UISettings.SavedAtmosphere.Parent = Lighting
        end)
    end

    UISettings.SavedAtmosphere = nil
    UISettings.AtmosphereRemoved = false
    UISettings.KillAtmosphere = false
end

-- Reset non-shader options that can be changed by a preset.
-- Presets must not inherit FOV / aspect / Kill Atmosphere state
-- from the previously selected preset.
function _Yuki_ResetPresetUIState()
    -- Preserve FOV / Aspect Ratio when they are enabled.
    -- Other preset-external UI state is reset normally.
    _Yuki_RestoreKilledAtmosphere()

    if not UISettings.FOVEnabled then
        UISettings.FOVValue = 70
        if Camera then
            pcall(function()
                Camera.FieldOfView = 70
            end)
        end
    end

    if not UISettings.AspectEnabled then
        UISettings.AspectValue = 0.6
    end
end

--============================================================
-- ANTI LAG
-- Based on the Anti Lag implementation supplied by the user.
--============================================================

function _Yuki_ApplyAntiLagInstance(instance)
    if (instance:IsA("Part") or instance:IsA("MeshPart")) and instance.Transparency < 1 then
        if not instance:GetAttribute("OldMaterial") then
            instance:SetAttribute("OldMaterial", instance.Material.Name)
        end

        instance.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic

        if instance:IsA("MeshPart") then
            if instance.TextureID and instance.TextureID ~= "" and not instance:GetAttribute("OldTextureID") then
                instance:SetAttribute("OldTextureID", instance.TextureID)
                instance.TextureID = ""
            end
        end
    end
end

function _Yuki_ApplyAntiLag(enable)
    if enable then
        for _, asset in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
            _Yuki_ApplyAntiLagInstance(asset)
        end
    else
        for _, asset in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
            if asset:IsA("Part") or asset:IsA("MeshPart") then
                local oldMatName = asset:GetAttribute("OldMaterial")
                if oldMatName then
                    for _, enumItem in ipairs(Enum.Material:GetEnumItems()) do
                        if enumItem.Name == oldMatName then
                            asset.Material = enumItem
                            break
                        end
                    end
                    asset:SetAttribute("OldMaterial", nil)
                end

                if asset:IsA("MeshPart") then
                    local oldTex = asset:GetAttribute("OldTextureID")
                    if oldTex then
                        asset.TextureID = oldTex
                        asset:SetAttribute("OldTextureID", nil)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
end

Connect(workspace.DescendantAdded, function(child)
    if AntiLagEnabled then
        _Yuki_ApplyAntiLagInstance(child)
    end
end)

--============================================================
-- MOTION BLUR RENDER LOOP (with FOV & Aspect)
--============================================================

Connect(
    RunService.RenderStepped,
    function(deltaTime)
        if not Alive then return end

        -- FOV Changer
        if UISettings.FOVEnabled and Camera then
            Camera.FieldOfView = UISettings.FOVValue
        elseif not UISettings.FOVEnabled and Camera then
            Camera.FieldOfView = 70
        end

        -- Aspect Ratio
        if UISettings.AspectEnabled and Camera then
            local cf = Camera.CFrame
            Camera.CFrame = cf * CFrame.new(0,0,0, 1,0,0, 0,UISettings.AspectValue,0, 0,0,1)
        end

        -- Motion Blur
        if not ShaderState.MotionBlur then
            if MotionBlurEffect then
                _Yuki_StopMotionBlur()
            end
            return
        end

        local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
        if not camera then return end

        _Yuki_StartMotionBlur()

        if not LastCameraCFrame then
            LastCameraCFrame = camera.CFrame
            return
        end

        local current = camera.CFrame

        local positionDelta =
            (current.Position - LastCameraCFrame.Position).Magnitude

        local a1, b1, c1 = current:ToOrientation()
        local a2, b2, c2 = LastCameraCFrame:ToOrientation()

        local rotationDelta =
            math.abs(a1 - a2) +
            math.abs(b1 - b2) +
            math.abs(c1 - c2)

        local movement = positionDelta + rotationDelta * 18

        local target =
            Clamp(
                movement * ShaderState.MotionBlurStrength * 12,
                0,
                24
            )

        MotionBlurEffect.Size =
            MotionBlurEffect.Size +
            (target - MotionBlurEffect.Size) *
            Clamp(deltaTime * 12, 0, 1)

        LastCameraCFrame = current
    end
)

--============================================================
-- DEFAULT STATE RESET
--============================================================

function _Yuki_ResetShaderState()
    ShaderState.Saturation = 0
    ShaderState.Brightness = 0
    ShaderState.Contrast = 0

    ShaderState.Blur = 0

    ShaderState.BloomIntensity = 0
    ShaderState.BloomSize = 24
    ShaderState.BloomThreshold = 1

    ShaderState.SunRaysIntensity = 0
    ShaderState.SunRaysSpread = 1

    ShaderState.DOFFarIntensity = 0
    ShaderState.DOFNearIntensity = 0
    ShaderState.DOFFocusDistance = 10

    ShaderState.AtmosphereDensity = 0

    ShaderState.MotionBlur = false
    ShaderState.MotionBlurStrength = 0

    ShaderState.Vignette = false
    ShaderState.VignetteStrength = 0

    CurrentPreset =
        "Default"
end

--============================================================
-- LIVE EFFECT UPDATERS
--============================================================

function _Yuki_UpdateColorCorrection()
    local effect =
        Effects.ColorCorrection

    if not effect then
        return
    end

    effect.Saturation =
        ShaderState.Saturation

    effect.Brightness =
        ShaderState.Brightness

    effect.Contrast =
        ShaderState.Contrast
end

function _Yuki_UpdateBlur()
    local effect =
        Effects.Blur

    if not effect then
        return
    end

    effect.Size =
        ShaderState.Blur
end

function _Yuki_UpdateBloom()
    local effect =
        Effects.Bloom

    if not effect then
        return
    end

    effect.Intensity =
        ShaderState.BloomIntensity

    effect.Size =
        ShaderState.BloomSize

    effect.Threshold =
        ShaderState.BloomThreshold
end

function _Yuki_UpdateSunRays()
    local effect =
        Effects.SunRays

    if not effect then
        return
    end

    effect.Intensity =
        ShaderState.SunRaysIntensity

    effect.Spread =
        ShaderState.SunRaysSpread
end

function _Yuki_UpdateDOF()
    local effect =
        Effects.DepthOfField

    if not effect then
        return
    end

    effect.FarIntensity =
        ShaderState.DOFFarIntensity

    effect.NearIntensity =
        ShaderState.DOFNearIntensity

    effect.FocusDistance =
        ShaderState.DOFFocusDistance
end

function _Yuki_UpdateAtmosphere()
    local effect =
        Effects.Atmosphere

    if not effect then
        return
    end

    effect.Density =
        ShaderState.AtmosphereDensity

    effect.Color =
        ShaderState.AtmosphereColor
end

--============================================================
-- ENSURE EFFECT
--============================================================

function _Yuki_EnsureEffect(effectType)
    if Effects[effectType] then
        return Effects[effectType]
    end

    if effectType == "ColorCorrection" then

        return _Yuki_CreateEffect(
            "ColorCorrection",
            {
                Saturation =
                    ShaderState.Saturation,

                Brightness =
                    ShaderState.Brightness,

                Contrast =
                    ShaderState.Contrast
            }
        )

    elseif effectType == "Blur" then

        return _Yuki_CreateEffect(
            "Blur",
            {
                Size =
                    ShaderState.Blur
            }
        )

    elseif effectType == "Bloom" then

        return _Yuki_CreateEffect(
            "Bloom",
            {
                Intensity =
                    ShaderState.BloomIntensity,

                Size =
                    ShaderState.BloomSize,

                Threshold =
                    ShaderState.BloomThreshold
            }
        )

    elseif effectType == "SunRays" then

        return _Yuki_CreateEffect(
            "SunRays",
            {
                Intensity =
                    ShaderState.SunRaysIntensity,

                Spread =
                    ShaderState.SunRaysSpread
            }
        )

    elseif effectType == "DepthOfField" then

        return _Yuki_CreateEffect(
            "DepthOfField",
            {
                FarIntensity =
                    ShaderState.DOFFarIntensity,

                NearIntensity =
                    ShaderState.DOFNearIntensity,

                FocusDistance =
                    ShaderState.DOFFocusDistance
            }
        )

    elseif effectType == "Atmosphere" then

        return _Yuki_CreateEffect(
            "Atmosphere",
            {
                Density =
                    ShaderState.AtmosphereDensity,

                Color =
                    ShaderState.AtmosphereColor
            }
        )
    end

    return nil
end

--============================================================
-- LIVE SETTING CHANGE
--============================================================

function _Yuki_SetLiveSetting(
    key,
    value
)
    ShaderState[key] =
        value

    CurrentPreset =
        "Custom"

    EnginePreset.Text =
        "Preset: Custom"

    if key == "Saturation"
        or key == "Brightness"
        or key == "Contrast" then

        _Yuki_EnsureEffect(
            "ColorCorrection"
        )

        _Yuki_UpdateColorCorrection()

    elseif key == "Blur" then

        if value <= 0 then

            SafeDestroy(
                Effects.Blur
            )

            Effects.Blur =
                nil

        else

            _Yuki_EnsureEffect(
                "Blur"
            )

            _Yuki_UpdateBlur()
        end

    elseif key == "BloomIntensity"
        or key == "BloomSize"
        or key == "BloomThreshold" then

        if ShaderState.BloomIntensity <= 0 then

            SafeDestroy(
                Effects.Bloom
            )

            Effects.Bloom =
                nil

        else

            _Yuki_EnsureEffect(
                "Bloom"
            )

            _Yuki_UpdateBloom()
        end

    elseif key == "SunRaysIntensity"
        or key == "SunRaysSpread" then

        if ShaderState.SunRaysIntensity <= 0 then

            SafeDestroy(
                Effects.SunRays
            )

            Effects.SunRays =
                nil

        else

            _Yuki_EnsureEffect(
                "SunRays"
            )

            _Yuki_UpdateSunRays()
        end

    elseif key == "DOFFarIntensity"
        or key == "DOFNearIntensity"
        or key == "DOFFocusDistance" then

        if ShaderState.DOFFarIntensity <= 0
            and ShaderState.DOFNearIntensity <= 0 then

            SafeDestroy(
                Effects.DepthOfField
            )

            Effects.DepthOfField =
                nil

        else

            _Yuki_EnsureEffect(
                "DepthOfField"
            )

            _Yuki_UpdateDOF()
        end

    elseif key == "AtmosphereDensity" then

        if value <= 0 then

            SafeDestroy(
                Effects.Atmosphere
            )

            Effects.Atmosphere =
                nil

        else

            _Yuki_EnsureEffect(
                "Atmosphere"
            )

            _Yuki_UpdateAtmosphere()
        end

    elseif key == "MotionBlurStrength" then

        if value <= 0 then

            ShaderState.MotionBlur =
                false

            _Yuki_StopMotionBlur()

        else

            ShaderState.MotionBlur =
                true

            _Yuki_StartMotionBlur()
        end

    elseif key == "VignetteStrength" then

        if value <= 0 then

            ShaderState.Vignette =
                false

            _Yuki_DestroyVignette()

        else

            ShaderState.Vignette =
                true

            _Yuki_UpdateVignette()
        end
    end
end

local SadlyPresetNames = {
    "Sorrow Blue",
    "Rainy Tears",
    "Empty Room",
    "Broken Heart",
    "Lost Memory",
    "Lonely Night",
    "Cold Tears",
    "After Goodbye",
    "Faded Hope",
    "Deep Sadness",
    "Last Sunset",
    "Melancholy Violet",
    "Quiet Regret"
}

local function _Yuki_ApplySadlyPreset(presetName)
    local data = SadlyPresetData[presetName]
    if type(data) ~= "table" then
        return false
    end

    local lighting = data.lighting or {}
    local color = data.color or {}
    local bloom = data.bloom or {}
    local dof = data.dof or {}
    local atmosphere = data.atmosphere or {}
    local sunRays = data.sunRays or {}

    for property, value in pairs(lighting) do
        pcall(function()
            Lighting[property] = value
        end)
    end

    _Yuki_CreateEffect("ColorCorrection", {
        Brightness = color.Brightness or 0,
        Contrast = color.Contrast or 0,
        Saturation = color.Saturation or 0,
        TintColor = color.TintColor or Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
        Enabled = true
    })

    if bloom.Intensity and bloom.Intensity > 0 then
        _Yuki_CreateEffect("Bloom", {
            Intensity = bloom.Intensity,
            Size = bloom.Size or 24,
            Threshold = bloom.Threshold or 1,
            Enabled = true
        })
    end

    if dof.FarIntensity and dof.FarIntensity > 0 or dof.NearIntensity and dof.NearIntensity > 0 then
        _Yuki_CreateEffect("DepthOfField", {
            FarIntensity = dof.FarIntensity or 0,
            NearIntensity = dof.NearIntensity or 0,
            FocusDistance = dof.FocusDistance or 10,
            InFocusRadius = dof.InFocusRadius or 18,
            Enabled = true
        })
    end

    if atmosphere.Density and atmosphere.Density > 0 then
        _Yuki_CreateEffect("Atmosphere", {
            Density = atmosphere.Density,
            Offset = atmosphere.Offset or 0,
            Color = atmosphere.Color or Color3.fromRGB(100, 100, 110),
            Decay = atmosphere.Decay or Color3.fromRGB(70, 70, 80),
            Glare = atmosphere.Glare or 0,
            Haze = atmosphere.Haze or 0
        })
    end

    if sunRays.Intensity and sunRays.Intensity > 0 then
        _Yuki_CreateEffect("SunRays", {
            Intensity = sunRays.Intensity,
            Spread = sunRays.Spread or 1,
            Enabled = true
        })
    end

    if data.blur then
        _Yuki_CreateEffect("Blur", {Size = data.blur, Enabled = true})
    end

    ShaderState.Saturation = color.Saturation or 0
    ShaderState.Brightness = color.Brightness or 0
    ShaderState.Contrast = color.Contrast or 0
    ShaderState.Blur = data.blur or 0
    ShaderState.BloomIntensity = bloom.Intensity or 0
    ShaderState.BloomSize = bloom.Size or 24
    ShaderState.BloomThreshold = bloom.Threshold or 1
    ShaderState.SunRaysIntensity = sunRays.Intensity or 0
    ShaderState.SunRaysSpread = sunRays.Spread or 1
    ShaderState.DOFFarIntensity = dof.FarIntensity or 0
    ShaderState.DOFNearIntensity = dof.NearIntensity or 0
    ShaderState.DOFFocusDistance = dof.FocusDistance or 10
    ShaderState.AtmosphereDensity = atmosphere.Density or 0
    ShaderState.AtmosphereColor = atmosphere.Color or Color3.fromRGB(100, 100, 110)

    ShaderState.MotionBlur = data.motionBlur == true
    ShaderState.MotionBlurStrength = data.motionBlurStrength or 0
    if ShaderState.MotionBlur then
        _Yuki_StartMotionBlur()
    else
        _Yuki_StopMotionBlur()
    end

    ShaderState.Vignette = true
    ShaderState.VignetteStrength = data.vignette or 0.35
    _Yuki_UpdateVignette()

    return true
end

for _, presetName in ipairs(SadlyPresetNames) do
    Presets[presetName] = function()
        return _Yuki_ApplySadlyPreset(presetName)
    end
end

local ExtraJSONPresets = {}

pcall(function()
    ExtraJSONPresets = HttpService:JSONDecode(ExtraJSONPresetData)
end)

function _Yuki_JSONColor(value, fallback)
    if type(value) == "table" and #value >= 3 then
        return Color3.fromRGB(
            Clamp(tonumber(value[1]) or 255, 0, 255),
            Clamp(tonumber(value[2]) or 255, 0, 255),
            Clamp(tonumber(value[3]) or 255, 0, 255)
        )
    end
    return fallback or Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
end

function _Yuki_ApplyExtraJSONPreset(presetName)
    local data = ExtraJSONPresets[presetName]
    if type(data) ~= "table" then
        return false
    end

    local lighting = data.lighting or {}
    local color = data.colorCorrection or {}
    local bloom = data.bloom or {}
    local dof = data.dof or {}
    local atmosphere = data.atmosphere or {}
    local sunRays = data.sunRays or {}

    pcall(function() if lighting.ClockTime ~= nil then Lighting.ClockTime = lighting.ClockTime end end)
    pcall(function() if lighting.Brightness ~= nil then Lighting.Brightness = lighting.Brightness end end)
    pcall(function() if lighting.ExposureCompensation ~= nil then Lighting.ExposureCompensation = lighting.ExposureCompensation end end)
    pcall(function() if lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale ~= nil then Lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale = lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale end end)
    pcall(function() if lighting.EnvironmentSpecularScale ~= nil then Lighting.EnvironmentSpecularScale = lighting.EnvironmentSpecularScale end end)
    pcall(function() if lighting.Ambient then Lighting.Ambient = _Yuki_JSONColor(lighting.Ambient) end end)
    pcall(function() if lighting.OutdoorAmbient then Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = _Yuki_JSONColor(lighting.OutdoorAmbient) end end)

    _Yuki_CreateEffect("ColorCorrection", {
        Brightness = tonumber(color.Brightness) or 0,
        Contrast = tonumber(color.Contrast) or 0,
        Saturation = tonumber(color.Saturation) or 0,
        TintColor = _Yuki_JSONColor(color.TintColor),
        Enabled = true
    })

    if tonumber(bloom.Intensity) and tonumber(bloom.Intensity) > 0 then
        _Yuki_CreateEffect("Bloom", {
            Intensity = tonumber(bloom.Intensity) or 0,
            Size = tonumber(bloom.Size) or 24,
            Threshold = tonumber(bloom.Threshold) or 1,
            Enabled = true
        })
    end

    if tonumber(dof.FarIntensity) and tonumber(dof.FarIntensity) > 0 or tonumber(dof.NearIntensity) and tonumber(dof.NearIntensity) > 0 then
        _Yuki_CreateEffect("DepthOfField", {
            FarIntensity = tonumber(dof.FarIntensity) or 0,
            NearIntensity = tonumber(dof.NearIntensity) or 0,
            FocusDistance = tonumber(dof.FocusDistance) or 10,
            InFocusRadius = tonumber(dof.InFocusRadius) or 10,
            Enabled = true
        })
    end

    if tonumber(atmosphere.Density) and tonumber(atmosphere.Density) > 0 then
        _Yuki_CreateEffect("Atmosphere", {
            Density = tonumber(atmosphere.Density) or 0,
            Offset = tonumber(atmosphere.Offset) or 0,
            Color = _Yuki_JSONColor(atmosphere.Color),
            Decay = _Yuki_JSONColor(atmosphere.Decay),
            Glare = tonumber(atmosphere.Glare) or 0,
            Haze = tonumber(atmosphere.Haze) or 0
        })
    end

    if tonumber(sunRays.Intensity) and tonumber(sunRays.Intensity) > 0 then
        _Yuki_CreateEffect("SunRays", {
            Intensity = tonumber(sunRays.Intensity) or 0,
            Spread = tonumber(sunRays.Spread) or 1,
            Enabled = true
        })
    end

    ShaderState.Saturation = tonumber(color.Saturation) or 0
    ShaderState.Brightness = tonumber(color.Brightness) or 0
    ShaderState.Contrast = tonumber(color.Contrast) or 0
    ShaderState.Blur = 0
    ShaderState.BloomIntensity = tonumber(bloom.Intensity) or 0
    ShaderState.BloomSize = tonumber(bloom.Size) or 24
    ShaderState.BloomThreshold = tonumber(bloom.Threshold) or 1
    ShaderState.SunRaysIntensity = tonumber(sunRays.Intensity) or 0
    ShaderState.SunRaysSpread = tonumber(sunRays.Spread) or 1
    ShaderState.DOFFarIntensity = tonumber(dof.FarIntensity) or 0
    ShaderState.DOFNearIntensity = tonumber(dof.NearIntensity) or 0
    ShaderState.DOFFocusDistance = tonumber(dof.FocusDistance) or 10
    ShaderState.AtmosphereDensity = tonumber(atmosphere.Density) or 0
    ShaderState.AtmosphereColor = _Yuki_JSONColor(atmosphere.Color, Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200))

    ShaderState.Vignette = (tonumber(data.vignette) or 0) > 0
    ShaderState.VignetteStrength = Clamp(tonumber(data.vignette) or 0, 0, 1)
    _Yuki_UpdateVignette()

    return true
end

local ExtraJSONPresetNames = {}
for presetName in pairs(ExtraJSONPresets) do
    table.insert(ExtraJSONPresetNames, presetName)
end
table.sort(ExtraJSONPresetNames)


-- These are the exact custom skybox ids used by the donor script.
local PShadeCustomSky = {
    SkyboxBk = "rbxassetid://9544505500",
    SkyboxDn = "rbxassetid://9544547905",
    SkyboxFt = "rbxassetid://9544504852",
    SkyboxLf = "rbxassetid://9544547694",
    SkyboxRt = "rbxassetid://9544547542",
    SkyboxUp = "rbxassetid://9544547398"
}

-- Keep the original world so Default can restore it exactly like the
-- donor's initial backup state.
local OriginalWorldState = {
    ClockTime = Lighting.ClockTime,
    Ambient = Lighting.Ambient,
    Brightness = Lighting.Brightness,
    ColorShift_Bottom = Lighting.ColorShift_Bottom,
    ColorShift_Top = Lighting.ColorShift_Top,
    EnvironmentDiffuseScale = Lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale,
    EnvironmentSpecularScale = Lighting.EnvironmentSpecularScale,
    GlobalShadows = Lighting.GlobalShadows,
    OutdoorAmbient = Lighting.OutdoorAmbient,
    GeographicLatitude = Lighting.GeographicLatitude,
    ExposureCompensation = Lighting.ExposureCompensation,
    FogEnd = Lighting.FogEnd,
    FogColor = Lighting.FogColor,
    FogStart = Lighting.FogStart
}

local OriginalSkyState = nil
local OriginalSky = Lighting:FindFirstChildOfClass("Sky")
if OriginalSky then
    OriginalSkyState = {
        SkyboxBk = OriginalSky.SkyboxBk,
        SkyboxDn = OriginalSky.SkyboxDn,
        SkyboxFt = OriginalSky.SkyboxFt,
        SkyboxLf = OriginalSky.SkyboxLf,
        SkyboxRt = OriginalSky.SkyboxRt,
        SkyboxUp = OriginalSky.SkyboxUp
    }
end

local OriginalCloudState = nil
local OriginalClouds = workspace.Terrain:FindFirstChildOfClass("Clouds")
if OriginalClouds then
    OriginalCloudState = {
        Cover = OriginalClouds.Cover,
        Density = OriginalClouds.Density,
        Color = OriginalClouds.Color
    }
end

function _Yuki_LoadPShadePreset(presetName)
    if PShadeCache[presetName] ~= nil then
        return PShadeCache[presetName]
    end

    local filename = PShadeSources[presetName]
    if not filename then
        return nil
    end

    local ok, result = pcall(function()
        local source = game:HttpGet(PShadeBaseUrl .. filename)
        local loader = loadstring(source)
        if not loader then
            error("loadstring is unavailable")
        end
        local value = loader()
        if type(value) ~= "table" then
            error("preset did not return a table")
        end
        return value
    end)

    if not ok then
        PShadeCache[presetName] = false
        return nil
    end

    PShadeCache[presetName] = result
    return result
end

function _Yuki_GetSky()
    local sky = Lighting:FindFirstChildOfClass("Sky")
    if not sky then
        sky = Instance.new("Sky")
        sky.Parent = Lighting
    end
    return sky
end

function _Yuki_GetClouds()
    local clouds = workspace.Terrain:FindFirstChildOfClass("Clouds")
    if not clouds then
        clouds = Instance.new("Clouds")
        clouds.Parent = workspace.Terrain
    end
    return clouds
end

function _Yuki_ApplyPShadeWorld(light)
    -- Donor script uses its custom skybox while a shader is active.
    local sky = _Yuki_GetSky()
    for property, value in pairs(PShadeCustomSky) do
        pcall(function()
            sky[property] = value
        end)
    end

    local worldMap = {
        Ambient = "yfbghj",
        ClockTime = "tgvbyd",
        GeographicLatitude = "ghuybhuyhj",
        Brightness = "khnbfth",
        ColorShift_Bottom = "hgyghkg",
        ColorShift_Top = "yfbhjku",
        EnvironmentDiffuseScale = "ygyyfgvhbjytrt",
        EnvironmentSpecularScale = "sdfcddc",
        GlobalShadows = "hgnujuu7thgr",
        OutdoorAmbient = "hyhnngtf",
        ExposureCompensation = "hdfr7thgr"
    }

    for property, key in pairs(worldMap) do
        local value = light[key]
        if value ~= nil then
            pcall(function()
                Lighting[property] = value
            end)
        end
    end

    -- Exact PShade fog behavior.
    pcall(function()
        Lighting.FogEnd = math.huge
        Lighting.FogStart = math.huge
        Lighting.FogColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    end)

    local clouds = _Yuki_GetClouds()
    pcall(function() clouds.Cover = light["gyhgtg"] or 0 end)
    pcall(function() clouds.Density = light["ygbhggv"] or 0 end)
    pcall(function() clouds.Color = light["jghbjhgyfd"] or Color3.new(1, 1, 1) end)
end

function _Yuki_ApplyPShadePreset(presetName)
    local light = _Yuki_LoadPShadePreset(presetName)
    if not light then
        EngineStatus.Text = "ERROR"
        return false
    end

    -- PShade defaults: ColorCorrection ON, Bloom ON, DOF ON,
    -- Blur OFF, SunRays OFF. Values are still loaded exactly.
    local colorCorrection = _Yuki_CreateEffect("ColorCorrection", {
        Brightness = light["fhnchvhfjsd"] or 0,
        Contrast = light["ugtbbjhygt"] or 0,
        Saturation = light["tfbghuugbnjhg"] or 0,
        TintColor = light["fvrtccvghghj"] or Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
        Enabled = true
    })

    local bloom = _Yuki_CreateEffect("Bloom", {
        Intensity = light["jnfdhbnfcvh"] or 0,
        Size = light["fvtyghj"] or 24,
        Threshold = light["ygbhnj"] or 1,
        Enabled = true
    })

    local blur = _Yuki_CreateEffect("Blur", {
        Size = light["njnfg"] or 0,
        Enabled = false
    })

    local depth = _Yuki_CreateEffect("DepthOfField", {
        FarIntensity = light["jdfkd"] or 0,
        FocusDistance = light["fvgsdfg"] or 10,
        InFocusRadius = light["sdkvkflv"] or 10,
        NearIntensity = light["hbjhd"] or 0,
        Enabled = true
    })

    local sunRays = _Yuki_CreateEffect("SunRays", {
        Intensity = light["srayIntensity"] or 0,
        Spread = light["sraySpread"] or 1,
        Enabled = false
    })

    local atmosphere = _Yuki_CreateEffect("Atmosphere", {
        Density = light["shdbsnjfc"] or 0,
        Offset = light["skdjfkdm"] or 0,
        Color = light["sjdjncdjf"] or Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
        Decay = light["efjdjfk"] or Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
        Glare = light["sejfd"] or 0,
        Haze = light["jddfjsd"] or 0
    })

    -- Keep references explicit so later slider/toggle synchronization uses
    -- the exact imported values.
    Effects.ColorCorrection = colorCorrection
    Effects.Bloom = bloom
    Effects.Blur = blur
    Effects.DepthOfField = depth
    Effects.SunRays = sunRays
    Effects.Atmosphere = atmosphere

    _Yuki_ApplyPShadeWorld(light)

    ShaderState.Saturation = colorCorrection.Saturation
    ShaderState.Brightness = colorCorrection.Brightness
    ShaderState.Contrast = colorCorrection.Contrast
    ShaderState.Blur = light["njnfg"] or 0
    ShaderState.BloomIntensity = light["jnfdhbnfcvh"] or 0
    ShaderState.BloomSize = light["fvtyghj"] or 24
    ShaderState.BloomThreshold = light["ygbhnj"] or 1
    ShaderState.SunRaysIntensity = 0
    ShaderState.SunRaysSpread = 1
    ShaderState.DOFFarIntensity = light["jdfkd"] or 0
    ShaderState.DOFNearIntensity = light["hbjhd"] or 0
    ShaderState.DOFFocusDistance = light["fvgsdfg"] or 10
    ShaderState.AtmosphereDensity = light["shdbsnjfc"] or 0
    ShaderState.AtmosphereColor = light["sjdjncdjf"] or Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200)

    return true
end

function _Yuki_RestoreOriginalWorld()
    for property, value in pairs(OriginalWorldState) do
        pcall(function()
            Lighting[property] = value
        end)
    end

    local sky = Lighting:FindFirstChildOfClass("Sky")
    if sky then
        if OriginalSkyState then
            for property, value in pairs(OriginalSkyState) do
                pcall(function()
                    sky[property] = value
                end)
            end
        else
            SafeDestroy(sky)
        end
    end

    local clouds = workspace.Terrain:FindFirstChildOfClass("Clouds")
    if clouds then
        if OriginalCloudState then
            for property, value in pairs(OriginalCloudState) do
                pcall(function()
                    clouds[property] = value
                end)
            end
        else
            SafeDestroy(clouds)
        end
    end
end

local RefreshAllSliders

--============================================================
-- APPLY PRESET
--============================================================

function _Yuki_SyncStateFromLoadedPreset()
    local colorCorrection =
        Effects.ColorCorrection

    if colorCorrection then
        ShaderState.Saturation =
            colorCorrection.Saturation

        ShaderState.Brightness =
            colorCorrection.Brightness

        ShaderState.Contrast =
            colorCorrection.Contrast
    else
        ShaderState.Saturation = 0
        ShaderState.Brightness = 0
        ShaderState.Contrast = 0
    end

    if Effects.Blur then
        ShaderState.Blur =
            Effects.Blur.Size
    else
        ShaderState.Blur = 0
    end

    if Effects.Bloom then
        ShaderState.BloomIntensity =
            Effects.Bloom.Intensity

        ShaderState.BloomSize =
            Effects.Bloom.Size

        ShaderState.BloomThreshold =
            Effects.Bloom.Threshold
    else
        ShaderState.BloomIntensity = 0
        ShaderState.BloomSize = 24
        ShaderState.BloomThreshold = 1
    end

    if Effects.SunRays then
        ShaderState.SunRaysIntensity =
            Effects.SunRays.Intensity

        ShaderState.SunRaysSpread =
            Effects.SunRays.Spread
    else
        ShaderState.SunRaysIntensity = 0
        ShaderState.SunRaysSpread = 1
    end

    if Effects.DepthOfField then
        ShaderState.DOFFarIntensity =
            Effects.DepthOfField.FarIntensity

        ShaderState.DOFNearIntensity =
            Effects.DepthOfField.NearIntensity

        ShaderState.DOFFocusDistance =
            Effects.DepthOfField.FocusDistance
    else
        ShaderState.DOFFarIntensity = 0
        ShaderState.DOFNearIntensity = 0
        ShaderState.DOFFocusDistance = 10
    end

    if Effects.Atmosphere then
        ShaderState.AtmosphereDensity =
            Effects.Atmosphere.Density

        ShaderState.AtmosphereColor =
            Effects.Atmosphere.Color
    else
        ShaderState.AtmosphereDensity = 0
    end
end

function _Yuki_ApplyPreset(
    presetName
)
    local preset =
        Presets[presetName]

    if not preset then
        return
    end

    -- IMPORTANT:
    -- Imported PShade presets directly modify Lighting, Fog, Sky and Clouds.
    -- Previously those world changes survived when switching to another
    -- preset, because only YukiShader_* instances were destroyed.
    --
    -- Restore the original world FIRST, then remove our shader effects,
    -- then apply the new preset from a clean baseline.
    _Yuki_ResetPresetUIState()
    _Yuki_RestoreOriginalWorld()

    _Yuki_DestroyShaderEffects()
    _Yuki_DestroyVignette()
    _Yuki_StopMotionBlur()
    _Yuki_ResetShaderState()

    local ok, result =
        pcall(
            preset
        )

    if not ok or result == false then
        CurrentPreset =
            "Default"
        EngineStatus.Text = "ERROR"
        return
    end

    CurrentPreset =
        presetName

    _Yuki_SyncStateFromLoadedPreset()

    EnginePreset.Text =
        "Preset: "
        .. presetName

    EngineStatus.Text =
        "ACTIVE"

    if RefreshAllSliders then
        RefreshAllSliders()
    end

    if RefreshUIToggles then
        RefreshUIToggles()
    end
end

--============================================================
-- PRESET UI HELPERS
--============================================================

function _Yuki_CreatePresetButton(
    parent,
    presetName,
    layoutOrder
)
    local button =
        Instance.new("TextButton")

    button.Name =
        "Preset_" .. presetName

    button.LayoutOrder =
        layoutOrder

    button.BackgroundColor3 =
        Color3.fromRGB(
            250,
            247,
            248
        )

    button.BackgroundTransparency =
        0.02

    button.BorderSizePixel =
        0

    button.AutoButtonColor =
        false

    button.Text =
        ""

    button.ZIndex =
        23

    button.Parent =
        parent

    AddCorner(
        button,
        13
    )

    local stroke =
        AddStroke(
            button,
            CONFIG.PurpleLight,
            0.89,
            1
        )

    local accent =
        Instance.new("Frame")

    accent.Position =
        UDim2.fromOffset(
            9,
            9
        )

    accent.Size =
        UDim2.fromOffset(
            4,
            37
        )

    accent.BackgroundColor3 =
        CONFIG.Purple

    accent.BorderSizePixel =
        0

    accent.ZIndex =
        24

    accent.Parent =
        button

    AddCorner(
        accent,
        5
    )

    local title =
        Instance.new("TextLabel")

    title.Position =
        UDim2.fromOffset(
            23,
            7
        )

    title.Size =
        UDim2.new(
            1,
            -31,
            0,
            19
        )

    title.BackgroundTransparency =
        1

    title.Font =
        Enum.Font.GothamSemibold

    title.Text =
        presetName

    title.TextSize =
        10

    title.TextColor3 =
        CONFIG.Text

    title.TextTruncate =
        Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd

    title.TextXAlignment =
        Enum.TextXAlignment.Left

    title.ZIndex =
        25

    title.Parent =
        button

    local subtitle =
        Instance.new("TextLabel")

    subtitle.Position =
        UDim2.fromOffset(
            23,
            26
        )

    subtitle.Size =
        UDim2.new(
            1,
            -31,
            0,
            17
        )

    subtitle.BackgroundTransparency =
        1

    subtitle.Font =
        Enum.Font.Gotham

    subtitle.Text =
        "Click to apply"

    if presetName == "Yuki TikTok Preset" then
        subtitle.Text =
            "TikTok shader preset"
    end

    subtitle.TextSize =
        8

    subtitle.TextColor3 =
        CONFIG.Muted

    subtitle.TextXAlignment =
        Enum.TextXAlignment.Left

    subtitle.ZIndex =
        25

    subtitle.Parent =
        button

    Connect(
        button.MouseButton1Click,
        function()
            _Yuki_ApplyPreset(
                presetName
            )

            Tween(
                button,
                {
                    BackgroundColor3 =
                        CONFIG.Purple
                },
                0.12,
                Enum.EasingStyle.Sine
            )

            task.delay(
                0.13,
                function()
                    if not Alive then
                        return
                    end

                    Tween(
                        button,
                        {
                            BackgroundColor3 =
                                Color3.fromRGB(
                                    250,
                                    247,
                                    248
                                )
                        },
                        0.35,
                        Enum.EasingStyle.Sine
                    )
                end
            )
        end
    )

    return button
end

function _Yuki_CreatePresetSection(
    titleText,
    presetList,
    layoutOrder
)
    local section =
        Instance.new("Frame")

    section.Name =
        "Section_" .. titleText

    section.LayoutOrder =
        layoutOrder

    section.Size =
        UDim2.new(
            1,
            -6,
            0,
            0
        )

    section.AutomaticSize =
        Enum.AutomaticSize.Y

    section.BackgroundTransparency =
        1

    section.BorderSizePixel =
        0

    section.ZIndex =
        23

    section.Parent =
        PresetScroll

    local header =
        Instance.new("TextLabel")

    header.Size =
        UDim2.new(
            1,
            0,
            0,
            26
        )

    header.BackgroundTransparency =
        1

    header.Font =
        Enum.Font.GothamBold

    header.Text =
        titleText

    header.TextSize =
        12

    header.TextColor3 =
        CONFIG.PurpleLight

    header.TextXAlignment =
        Enum.TextXAlignment.Left

    header.ZIndex =
        24

    header.Parent =
        section

    local grid =
        Instance.new("Frame")

    grid.Position =
        UDim2.fromOffset(
            0,
            30
        )

    grid.Size =
        UDim2.new(
            1,
            0,
            0,
            0
        )

    grid.AutomaticSize =
        Enum.AutomaticSize.Y

    grid.BackgroundTransparency =
        1

    grid.BorderSizePixel =
        0

    grid.ZIndex =
        23

    grid.Parent =
        section

    local layout =
        Instance.new("UIGridLayout")

    layout.CellSize =
        UDim2.new(
            0.333,
            -8,
            0,
            55
        )

    layout.CellPadding =
        UDim2.fromOffset(
            8,
            8
        )

    layout.SortOrder =
        Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder

    layout.Parent =
        grid

    for index, presetName in ipairs(
        presetList
    ) do
        _Yuki_CreatePresetButton(
            grid,
            presetName,
            index
        )
    end

    return section
end

        0,

    )

        "AbsoluteContentSize"
    ),
    function()
        PresetScroll.CanvasSize =
            UDim2.fromOffset(
                0,
                PresetScrollLayout.AbsoluteContentSize.Y
                    + 10
            )
    end
)

    UDim2.fromOffset(

        PresetScrollLayout.AbsoluteContentSize.Y
            + 10
    )

--============================================================
-- SLIDER CREATOR
--============================================================

function _Yuki_CreateSlider(
    parent,
    definition,
    index
)

    local row =
        Instance.new("Frame")

    row.Name =
        definition.Key

    row.LayoutOrder =
        index

    row.Position =
        UDim2.fromOffset(
            8,
            (index - 1) * 65
        )

    row.Size =
        UDim2.new(
            1,
            -16,
            0,
            58
        )

    row.BackgroundColor3 =
        Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)

    row.BackgroundTransparency =
        0.02

    row.BorderSizePixel =
        0

    row.ZIndex =
        23

    row.Parent =
        parent

    AddCorner(
        row,
        13
    )

    AddStroke(
        row,
        CONFIG.PurpleLight,
        0.91,
        1
    )

    local label =
        Instance.new("TextLabel")

    label.Position =
        UDim2.fromOffset(
            13,
            7
        )

    label.Size =
        UDim2.new(
            1,
            -100,
            0,
            18
        )

    label.BackgroundTransparency =
        1

    label.Font =
        Enum.Font.GothamMedium

    label.Text =
        definition.Name

    label.TextSize =
        10

    label.TextColor3 =
        CONFIG.Text

    label.TextXAlignment =
        Enum.TextXAlignment.Left

    label.ZIndex =
        24

    label.Parent =
        row

    local valueLabel =
        Instance.new("TextLabel")

    valueLabel.AnchorPoint =
        Vector2.new(
            1,
            0
        )

    valueLabel.Position =
        UDim2.new(
            1,
            -13,
            0,
            7
        )

    valueLabel.Size =
        UDim2.fromOffset(
            70,
            18
        )

    valueLabel.BackgroundTransparency =
        1

    valueLabel.Font =
        Enum.Font.GothamSemibold

    valueLabel.TextSize =
        9

    valueLabel.TextColor3 =
        CONFIG.PurpleLight

    valueLabel.TextXAlignment =
        Enum.TextXAlignment.Right

    valueLabel.ZIndex =
        24

    valueLabel.Parent =
        row

    local track =
        Instance.new("Frame")

    track.Position =
        UDim2.fromOffset(
            13,
            37
        )

    track.Size =
        UDim2.new(
            1,
            -26,
            0,
            6
        )

    track.BackgroundColor3 =
        Color3.fromRGB(232, 228, 230)

    track.BorderSizePixel =
        0

    track.ZIndex =
        24

    track.Parent =
        row

    AddCorner(
        track,
        10
    )

    local fill =
        Instance.new("Frame")

    fill.Size =
        UDim2.fromScale(
            0,
            1
        )

    fill.BackgroundColor3 =
        CONFIG.Purple

    fill.BorderSizePixel =
        0

    fill.ZIndex =
        25

    fill.Parent =
        track

    AddCorner(
        fill,
        10
    )

    local knob =
        Instance.new("Frame")

    knob.AnchorPoint =
        Vector2.new(
            0.5,
            0.5
        )

    knob.Position =
        UDim2.fromScale(
            0,
            0.5
        )

    knob.Size =
        UDim2.fromOffset(
            12,
            12
        )

    knob.BackgroundColor3 =
        CONFIG.White

    knob.BorderSizePixel =
        0

    knob.ZIndex =
        26

    knob.Parent =
        track

    AddCorner(
        knob,
        99
    )

    local dragging =
        false

    local function SetUIValue(
        value,
        instant
    )
        local normalized =
            (
                value
                -
                definition.Min
            )
            /
            (
                definition.Max
                -
                definition.Min
            )

        normalized =
            Clamp(
                normalized,
                0,
                1
            )

        valueLabel.Text =
            string.format(
                "%.2f",
                value
            )

        if instant then

            fill.Size =
                UDim2.fromScale(
                    normalized,
                    1
                )

            knob.Position =
                UDim2.fromScale(
                    normalized,
                    0.5
                )

        else

            Tween(
                fill,
                {
                    Size =
                        UDim2.fromScale(
                            normalized,
                            1
                        )
                },
                0.08,
                Enum.EasingStyle.Sine
            )

            Tween(
                knob,
                {
                    Position =
                        UDim2.fromScale(
                            normalized,
                            0.5
                        )
                },
                0.08,
                Enum.EasingStyle.Sine
            )
        end
    end

    local function UpdateFromMouse(
        mouseX
    )
        local trackWidth =
            math.max(
                track.AbsoluteSize.X,
                1
            )

        local normalized =
            Clamp(
                (
                    mouseX
                    -
                    track.AbsolutePosition.X
                )
                /
                trackWidth,
                0,
                1
            )

        local rawValue =
            definition.Min
            +
            (
                definition.Max
                -
                definition.Min
            )
            *
            normalized

        local value =
            math.round(
                rawValue
                /
                definition.Step
            )
            *
            definition.Step

        value =
            Clamp(
                value,
                definition.Min,
                definition.Max
            )

        ShaderState[
            definition.Key
        ] =
            value

        SetUIValue(
            value,
            false
        )

        _Yuki_SetLiveSetting(
            definition.Key,
            value
        )
    end

    Connect(
        track.InputBegan,
        function(input)

            if input.UserInputType ==
                Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then

                dragging =
                    true

                UpdateFromMouse(
                    input.Position.X
                )
            end
        end
    )

    Connect(
        UserInputService.InputChanged,
        function(input)

            if not dragging then
                return
            end

            if input.UserInputType ==
                Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement then

                UpdateFromMouse(
                    input.Position.X
                )
            end
        end
    )

    Connect(
        UserInputService.InputEnded,
        function(input)

            if input.UserInputType ==
                Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then

                dragging =
                    false
            end
        end
    )

    SliderUI[
        definition.Key
    ] = {
        SetValue =
            SetUIValue
    }

    SetUIValue(
        definition.Default,
        true
    )
end

for index, definition in ipairs(
    SettingDefinitions
) do

    _Yuki_CreateSlider(
        SettingsScroll,
        definition,
        index
    )
end

--============================================================
-- UPDATE ALL SLIDER UI
--============================================================

RefreshAllSliders = function()
    for _, definition in ipairs(
        SettingDefinitions
    ) do

        local data =
            SliderUI[
                definition.Key
            ]

        if data then

            local value =
                ShaderState[
                    definition.Key
                ]

            if value == nil then
                value =
                    definition.Default
            end

            data.SetValue(
                value,
                true
            )
        end
    end
end

SettingsScroll.CanvasSize =
    UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)

--============================================================
-- TOGGLE CREATOR (existing)

local ToggleUI = {}

function _Yuki_CreateToggle(
    parent,
    name,
    index,
    getter,
    setter
)

    local y =
        #SettingDefinitions
        * 65
        + 8
        + (
            index - 1
        ) * 53

    local row =
        Instance.new("TextButton")

    row.Name =
        name

    row.LayoutOrder =
        50 + index

    row.Position =
        UDim2.fromOffset(
            8,
            y
        )

    row.Size =
        UDim2.new(
            1,
            -16,
            0,
            45
        )

    row.BackgroundColor3 =
        Color3.fromRGB(
            54,
            39,
            29
        )

    row.BackgroundTransparency =
        0.02

    row.BorderSizePixel =
        0

    row.AutoButtonColor =
        false

    row.Text =
        ""

    row.ZIndex =
        23

    row.Parent =
        parent

    AddCorner(
        row,
        13
    )

    AddStroke(
        row,
        CONFIG.PurpleLight,
        0.91,
        1
    )

    local title =
        Instance.new("TextLabel")

    title.Position =
        UDim2.fromOffset(
            13,
            0
        )

    title.Size =
        UDim2.new(
            1,
            -85,
            1,
            0
        )

    title.BackgroundTransparency =
        1

    title.Font =
        Enum.Font.GothamMedium

    title.Text =
        name

    title.TextSize =
        10

    title.TextColor3 =
        CONFIG.Text

    title.TextXAlignment =
        Enum.TextXAlignment.Left

    title.ZIndex =
        24

    title.Parent =
        row

    local switch =
        Instance.new("Frame")

    switch.AnchorPoint =
        Vector2.new(
            1,
            0.5
        )

    switch.Position =
        UDim2.new(
            1,
            -13,
            0.5,
            0
        )

    switch.Size =
        UDim2.fromOffset(
            42,
            20
        )

    switch.BackgroundColor3 =
        Color3.fromRGB(232, 228, 230)

    switch.BackgroundTransparency =
        0.78

    switch.BorderSizePixel =
        0

    switch.ZIndex =
        24

    switch.Parent =
        row

    AddCorner(
        switch,
        99
    )

    local knob =
        Instance.new("Frame")

    knob.Position =
        UDim2.fromOffset(
            2,
            2
        )

    knob.Size =
        UDim2.fromOffset(
            16,
            16
        )

    knob.BackgroundColor3 =
        CONFIG.White

    knob.BorderSizePixel =
        0

    knob.ZIndex =
        25

    knob.Parent =
        switch

    AddCorner(
        knob,
        99
    )

    local function Refresh()
        local state =
            getter()

        if state then

            Tween(
                switch,
                {
                    BackgroundColor3 =
                        CONFIG.Purple
                },
                0.18,
                Enum.EasingStyle.Sine
            )

            Tween(
                knob,
                {
                    Position =
                        UDim2.fromOffset(
                            24,
                            2
                        )
                },
                0.22,
                Enum.EasingStyle.Back
            )

        else

            Tween(
                switch,
                {
                    BackgroundColor3 =
                        Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),

                    BackgroundTransparency =
                        0.02
                },
                0.18,
                Enum.EasingStyle.Sine
            )

            Tween(
                knob,
                {
                    Position =
                        UDim2.fromOffset(
                            2,
                            2
                        )
                },
                0.22,
                Enum.EasingStyle.Back
            )
        end
    end

    Connect(
        row.MouseButton1Click,
        function()

            local newValue =
                not getter()

            setter(
                newValue
            )

            CurrentPreset =
                "Custom"

            EnginePreset.Text =
                "Preset: Custom"

            Refresh()
        end
    )

    ToggleUI[name] =
        Refresh

    Refresh()
end

-- Existing toggles: Motion Blur and Vignette
_Yuki_CreateToggle(
    SettingsScroll,
    "Motion Blur",
    1,

    function()
        return ShaderState.MotionBlur
    end,

    function(value)

        ShaderState.MotionBlur =
            value

        ShaderState.MotionBlurStrength =
            math.max(
                ShaderState.MotionBlurStrength,
                0.15
            )

        if value then
            _Yuki_StartMotionBlur()
        else
            _Yuki_StopMotionBlur()
        end
    end
)

_Yuki_CreateToggle(
    SettingsScroll,
    "Vignette",
    2,

    function()
        return ShaderState.Vignette
    end,

    function(value)

        ShaderState.Vignette =
            value

        if value then

            ShaderState.VignetteStrength =
                math.max(
                    ShaderState.VignetteStrength,
                    0.30
                )

            _Yuki_UpdateVignette()

        else

            _Yuki_DestroyVignette()
        end
    end
)

--============================================================
-- UI TOGGLES & SLIDERS FOR FOV, ASPECT, KILL ATMOSPHERE
--============================================================

-- We need a separate counter for indexing these new toggles
local newToggleIndex = 3
function _Yuki_CreateUIToggle(name, getter, setter, explicitOrder)
    _Yuki_CreateToggle(SettingsScroll, name, newToggleIndex, getter, setter)
    local row = SettingsScroll:FindFirstChild(name)
    if row and explicitOrder then
        row.LayoutOrder = explicitOrder
    end
    newToggleIndex = newToggleIndex + 1
end


-- Build all transferred Visuals toggles in the new Effects page.
for index, effectName in ipairs(PortalVisualEffects) do
    _Yuki_CreateToggle(
        EffectsScroll,
        effectName,
        index,
        function()
            return _G.PortalVisuals[effectName]
        end,
        function(value)
            _G.PortalVisuals[effectName] = value
        end
    )

    local row = EffectsScroll:FindFirstChild(effectName)
    if row then
        row.LayoutOrder = index
    end
end

-- Rain Range slider (the original Visuals tab had this extra control).
function _Yuki_CreateEffectRangeSlider(effectName, titleText, minValue, maxValue, initialValue, configKey, syncKey)
    local frame = Instance.new("Frame")
    frame.Name = effectName .. "Range"
    frame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    frame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
    frame.Visible = false
    frame.ClipsDescendants = true
    frame.LayoutOrder = 100 + table.find(PortalVisualEffects, effectName)
    frame.ZIndex = 23
    frame.Parent = EffectsScroll

    local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    label.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(4, 0)
    label.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -4, 0, 20)
    label.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
    label.Text = titleText
    label.TextColor3 = CONFIG.Muted
    label.TextSize = 10
    label.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    label.ZIndex = 24
    label.Parent = frame

    local valueLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    valueLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    valueLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 4, 0, 0)
    valueLabel.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -8, 0, 20)
    valueLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    valueLabel.Text = tostring(initialValue)
    valueLabel.TextColor3 = CONFIG.Text
    valueLabel.TextSize = 10
    valueLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
    valueLabel.ZIndex = 24
    valueLabel.Parent = frame

    local track = Instance.new("Frame")
    track.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(232, 228, 230)
    track.BorderSizePixel = 0
    track.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(4, 25)
    track.Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 6)
    track.ZIndex = 24
    track.Parent = frame
    AddCorner(track, 99)

    local fill = Instance.new("Frame")
    fill.BackgroundColor3 = CONFIG.Purple
    fill.BorderSizePixel = 0
    fill.Size = UDim2.new((initialValue - minValue) / (maxValue - minValue), 0, 1, 0)
    fill.ZIndex = 25
    fill.Parent = track
    AddCorner(fill, 99)

    local thumb = Instance.new("Frame")
    thumb.BackgroundColor3 = CONFIG.White
    thumb.BorderSizePixel = 0
    thumb.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    thumb.Position = UDim2.new((initialValue - minValue) / (maxValue - minValue), 0, 0.5, 0)
    thumb.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 14)
    thumb.ZIndex = 26
    thumb.Parent = track
    AddCorner(thumb, 99)

    local dragging = false
    local current = initialValue

    local function setValue(value)
        current = math.clamp(math.floor(value + 0.5), minValue, maxValue)
        local alpha = (current - minValue) / (maxValue - minValue)
        fill.Size = UDim2.new(alpha, 0, 1, 0)
        thumb.Position = UDim2.new(alpha, 0, 0.5, 0)
        valueLabel.Text = tostring(current)
        local cfg = _G.PortalVisuals[syncKey]
        if type(cfg) == "table" then
            cfg[configKey] = current
        end
        local sync = _G.PortalVisuals[syncKey == "_rainConfig" and "_rainSync" or "_snowSync"]
        if type(sync) == "function" then
            sync()
        end
    end

    local function inputToValue(input)
        local width = math.max(track.AbsoluteSize.X, 1)
        local alpha = math.clamp((input.Position.X - track.AbsolutePosition.X) / width, 0, 1)
        return minValue + (maxValue - minValue) * alpha
    end

    Connect(track.InputBegan, function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            dragging = true
            setValue(inputToValue(input))
        end
    end)
    Connect(thumb.InputBegan, function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            dragging = true
        end
    end)
    Connect(UserInputService.InputChanged, function(input)
        if dragging and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
            setValue(inputToValue(input))
        end
    end)
    Connect(UserInputService.InputEnded, function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            dragging = false
        end
    end)

    local listeners = _G.PortalVisuals._listeners[effectName]
    if not listeners then
        listeners = {}
        _G.PortalVisuals._listeners[effectName] = listeners
    end
    table.insert(listeners, function(state)
        if state then
            frame.Visible = true
            frame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
            Tween(frame, {Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 43)}, 0.28, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
        else
            Tween(frame, {Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)}, 0.22, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.In)
            task.delay(0.22, function()
                if frame and frame.Parent and not _G.PortalVisuals[effectName] then
                    frame.Visible = false
                end
            end)
        end
    end)

    EffectRangeUI[effectName] = {
        SetValue = setValue
    }

    return frame
end

_Yuki_CreateEffectRangeSlider("Rain", "Rain Range", 15, 120, 45, "RAIN_RADIUS", "_rainConfig")
_Yuki_CreateEffectRangeSlider("Snowfall", "Snow Range", 15, 160, 60, "SpawnRadius", "_snowConfig")

EffectsScroll.CanvasSize = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)
