    "Enable FOV Changer",
    function() return UISettings.FOVEnabled end,
    function(value)
        UISettings.FOVEnabled = value
        if not value and Camera then
            Camera.FieldOfView = 70
        end
    end,
    100
)

-- FOV Slider (custom, because it's not in ShaderState)
do
    local definition = {
        Name = "FOV",
        Key = "FOVValue",
        Min = 1,
        Max = 120,
        Step = 1,
        Default = 70
    }
    -- use a modified slider that updates UISettings
    local function CreateUISlider(parent, def, indexOffset)
        -- reuse most of the slider code, but with custom callback
        local row = Instance.new("Frame")
        row.Name = def.Key
        row.LayoutOrder = 101
        row.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 0)
        row.Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 58)
        row.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
        row.BackgroundTransparency = 0.90
        row.BorderSizePixel = 0
        row.ZIndex = 23
        row.Parent = parent
        AddCorner(row, 13)
        AddStroke(row, CONFIG.PurpleLight, 0.91, 1)

        local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        label.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(13, 7)
        label.Size = UDim2.new(1, -100, 0, 18)
        label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
        label.Text = def.Name
        label.TextSize = 10
        label.TextColor3 = CONFIG.Text
        label.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        label.ZIndex = 24
        label.Parent = row

        local valueLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        valueLabel.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1,0)
        valueLabel.Position = UDim2.new(1, -13, 0, 7)
        valueLabel.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(70, 18)
        valueLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        valueLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold
        valueLabel.TextSize = 9
        valueLabel.TextColor3 = CONFIG.PurpleLight
        valueLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
        valueLabel.ZIndex = 24
        valueLabel.Parent = row

        local track = Instance.new("Frame")
        track.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(13, 37)
        track.Size = UDim2.new(1, -26, 0, 6)
        track.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(232, 228, 230)
        track.BorderSizePixel = 0
        track.ZIndex = 24
        track.Parent = row
        AddCorner(track, 10)

        local fill = Instance.new("Frame")
        fill.Size = UDim2.fromScale(0,1)
        fill.BackgroundColor3 = CONFIG.Purple
        fill.BorderSizePixel = 0
        fill.ZIndex = 25
        fill.Parent = track
        AddCorner(fill, 10)

        local knob = Instance.new("Frame")
        knob.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5,0.5)
        knob.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0,0.5)
        knob.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(12,12)
        knob.BackgroundColor3 = CONFIG.White
        knob.BorderSizePixel = 0
        knob.ZIndex = 26
        knob.Parent = track
        AddCorner(knob, 99)

        local dragging = false

        local function SetUIValue(value, instant)
            local normalized = (value - def.Min) / (def.Max - def.Min)
            normalized = Clamp(normalized, 0, 1)
            valueLabel.Text = string.format("%.2f", value)
            if instant then
                fill.Size = UDim2.fromScale(normalized, 1)
                knob.Position = UDim2.fromScale(normalized, 0.5)
            else
                Tween(fill, {Size = UDim2.fromScale(normalized, 1)}, 0.08, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine)
                Tween(knob, {Position = UDim2.fromScale(normalized, 0.5)}, 0.08, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine)
            end
        end

        local function UpdateFromMouse(mouseX)
            local trackWidth = math.max(track.AbsoluteSize.X, 1)
            local normalized = Clamp((mouseX - track.AbsolutePosition.X) / trackWidth, 0, 1)
            local rawValue = def.Min + (def.Max - def.Min) * normalized
            local value = math.round(rawValue / def.Step) * def.Step
            value = Clamp(value, def.Min, def.Max)
            UISettings[def.Key] = value
            SetUIValue(value, false)
            -- apply FOV immediately if enabled
            if UISettings.FOVEnabled and Camera then
                Camera.FieldOfView = value
            end
        end

        Connect(track.InputBegan, function(input)
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
                dragging = true
                UpdateFromMouse(input.Position.X)
            end
        end)

        Connect(UserInputService.InputChanged, function(input)
            if not dragging then return end
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement then
                UpdateFromMouse(input.Position.X)
            end
        end)

        Connect(UserInputService.InputEnded, function(input)
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
                dragging = false
            end
        end)

        -- store reference for later refresh
        if not SliderUI["FOVValue"] then
            SliderUI["FOVValue"] = { SetValue = SetUIValue }
        end
        SetUIValue(def.Default, true)
    end

    CreateUISlider(SettingsScroll, definition, 1)
end

_Yuki_CreateUIToggle(
    "Enable Aspect Ratio",
    function() return UISettings.AspectEnabled end,
    function(value)
        UISettings.AspectEnabled = value
    end,
    102
)

-- Aspect Ratio Slider
do
    local definition = {

        Key = "AspectValue",

        valueLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right

        track.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(232, 228, 230)

        fill.BackgroundColor3 = CONFIG.Purple

        knob.BackgroundColor3 = CONFIG.White
        knob.BorderSizePixel = 0
        knob.ZIndex = 26
        knob.Parent = track
        AddCorner(knob, 99)

        local dragging = false

        local function SetUIValue(value, instant)
            local normalized = (value - def.Min) / (def.Max - def.Min)
            normalized = Clamp(normalized, 0, 1)
            valueLabel.Text = string.format("%.2f", value)
            if instant then
                fill.Size = UDim2.fromScale(normalized, 1)
                knob.Position = UDim2.fromScale(normalized, 0.5)
            else
                Tween(fill, {Size = UDim2.fromScale(normalized, 1)}, 0.08, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine)
                Tween(knob, {Position = UDim2.fromScale(normalized, 0.5)}, 0.08, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine)
            end
        end

        local function UpdateFromMouse(mouseX)
            local trackWidth = math.max(track.AbsoluteSize.X, 1)
            local normalized = Clamp((mouseX - track.AbsolutePosition.X) / trackWidth, 0, 1)
            local rawValue = def.Min + (def.Max - def.Min) * normalized
            local value = math.round(rawValue / def.Step) * def.Step
            value = Clamp(value, def.Min, def.Max)
            UISettings[def.Key] = value
            SetUIValue(value, false)
        end

        Connect(track.InputBegan, function(input)
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
                dragging = true
                UpdateFromMouse(input.Position.X)
            end
        end)

        Connect(UserInputService.InputChanged, function(input)
            if not dragging then return end
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement then
                UpdateFromMouse(input.Position.X)
            end
        end)

        Connect(UserInputService.InputEnded, function(input)
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
                dragging = false
            end
        end)

        if not SliderUI["AspectValue"] then
            SliderUI["AspectValue"] = { SetValue = SetUIValue }
        end
        SetUIValue(def.Default, true)
    end

    CreateUISlider2(SettingsScroll, definition, 2)
end

-- Canvas is sized automatically by UIListLayout.
SettingsScroll.CanvasSize = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)

-- Refresh function for UI toggles (to sync when preset loaded)
RefreshUIToggles = function()
    for name, refresh in pairs(ToggleUI) do
        refresh()
    end
    -- also refresh FOV and Aspect sliders if needed
    if SliderUI["FOVValue"] then
        SliderUI["FOVValue"].SetValue(UISettings.FOVValue, true)
    end
    if SliderUI["AspectValue"] then
        SliderUI["AspectValue"].SetValue(UISettings.AspectValue, true)
    end
end

--============================================================
-- CUSTOM PRESETS
--============================================================

local CustomSection = nil
local CustomGrid = nil
local CustomCountLabel = nil
local CustomPlusButton = nil
local CustomEditorRefreshers = {}
local UpdateCustomSection

function _Yuki_CloneSimpleValue(value)
    if typeof(value) == "Color3" then
        return {__color = {value.R, value.G, value.B}}
    end
    if type(value) == "table" then
        local copy = {}
        for k, v in pairs(value) do
            copy[k] = _Yuki_CloneSimpleValue(v)
        end
        return copy
    end
    return value
end

function _Yuki_RestoreSimpleValue(value)
    if type(value) == "table" and value.__color then
        local c = value.__color
        return Color3.new(c[1] or 1, c[2] or 1, c[3] or 1)
    end
    if type(value) == "table" then
        local copy = {}
        for k, v in pairs(value) do
            copy[k] = _Yuki_RestoreSimpleValue(v)
        end
        return copy
    end
    return value
end

function _Yuki_SnapshotCustomState()
    local snapshot = {
        PresetName = CurrentPreset,
        ShaderState = _Yuki_CloneSimpleValue(ShaderState),
        UISettings = {
            FOVEnabled = UISettings.FOVEnabled,
            FOVValue = UISettings.FOVValue,
            AspectEnabled = UISettings.AspectEnabled,
            AspectValue = UISettings.AspectValue,
            KillAtmosphere = UISettings.KillAtmosphere
        },
        AntiLagEnabled = AntiLagEnabled,
        Effects = {},
        RainRadius = 45,
        SnowRadius = 60
    }

    for _, effectName in ipairs(PortalVisualEffects) do
        snapshot.Effects[effectName] = _G.PortalVisuals[effectName] == true
    end

    local rainConfig = _G.PortalVisuals._rainConfig
    if type(rainConfig) == "table" then
        snapshot.RainRadius = rainConfig.RAIN_RADIUS or snapshot.RainRadius
    end

    local snowConfig = _G.PortalVisuals._snowConfig
    if type(snowConfig) == "table" then
        snapshot.SnowRadius = snowConfig.SpawnRadius or snapshot.SnowRadius
    end

    return snapshot
end

function _Yuki_SaveCustomPresetsToFile()
    if type(writefile) ~= "function" then
        return
    end

    pcall(function()
        local serializable = {}
        for _, entry in ipairs(CustomPresets) do
            table.insert(serializable, {
                name = entry.name,
                data = _Yuki_CloneSimpleValue(entry.data)
            })
        end
        writefile(CustomPresetFile, HttpService:JSONEncode(serializable))
    end)
end

function _Yuki_LoadCustomPresetsFromFile()
    if type(readfile) ~= "function" then
        return
    end
    if type(isfile) == "function" and not isfile(CustomPresetFile) then
        return
    end

    local ok, content = pcall(function()
        return readfile(CustomPresetFile)
    end)
    if not ok or type(content) ~= "string" or content == "" then
        return
    end

    local decodedOk, decoded = pcall(function()
        return HttpService:JSONDecode(content)
    end)
    if not decodedOk or type(decoded) ~= "table" then
        return
    end

    for _, entry in ipairs(decoded) do
        if type(entry) == "table" and type(entry.name) == "string" and type(entry.data) == "table" then
            if #CustomPresets < 3 then
                entry.data = _Yuki_RestoreSimpleValue(entry.data)
                table.insert(CustomPresets, entry)
            end
        end
    end
end

function _Yuki_DisableAllPortalEffects()
    for _, effectName in ipairs(PortalVisualEffects) do
        if _G.PortalVisuals[effectName] then
            _G.PortalVisuals[effectName] = false
        end
    end
end

function _Yuki_ApplyCustomSnapshot(snapshot, presetLabel)
    if type(snapshot) ~= "table" then
        return false
    end

    _Yuki_RestoreKilledAtmosphere()
    _Yuki_RestoreOriginalWorld()
    _Yuki_DisableAllPortalEffects()
    _Yuki_DestroyShaderEffects()
    _Yuki_DestroyVignette()
    _Yuki_StopMotionBlur()
    _Yuki_ResetShaderState()

    local restoredShader = _Yuki_RestoreSimpleValue(snapshot.ShaderState or {})
    for key, value in pairs(restoredShader) do
        ShaderState[key] = value
    end

    UISettings.FOVEnabled = snapshot.UISettings and snapshot.UISettings.FOVEnabled == true or false
    UISettings.FOVValue = snapshot.UISettings and snapshot.UISettings.FOVValue or 70
    UISettings.AspectEnabled = snapshot.UISettings and snapshot.UISettings.AspectEnabled == true or false
    UISettings.AspectValue = snapshot.UISettings and snapshot.UISettings.AspectValue or 0.6
    UISettings.KillAtmosphere = snapshot.UISettings and snapshot.UISettings.KillAtmosphere == true or false

    _Yuki_EnsureEffect("ColorCorrection")
    _Yuki_EnsureEffect("Blur")
    _Yuki_EnsureEffect("Bloom")
    _Yuki_EnsureEffect("SunRays")
    _Yuki_EnsureEffect("DepthOfField")
    _Yuki_EnsureEffect("Atmosphere")

    _Yuki_UpdateColorCorrection()
    _Yuki_UpdateBlur()
    _Yuki_UpdateBloom()
    _Yuki_UpdateSunRays()
    _Yuki_UpdateDOF()
    _Yuki_UpdateAtmosphere()

    if ShaderState.MotionBlur then
        _Yuki_StartMotionBlur()
    else
        _Yuki_StopMotionBlur()
    end

    if ShaderState.Vignette then
        _Yuki_UpdateVignette()
    else
        _Yuki_DestroyVignette()
    end

    if UISettings.FOVEnabled and Camera then
        Camera.FieldOfView = UISettings.FOVValue
    elseif Camera then
        Camera.FieldOfView = 70
    end

    if UISettings.KillAtmosphere then
        _Yuki_ApplyKillAtmosphere(true)
    else
        _Yuki_ApplyKillAtmosphere(false)
    end

    AntiLagEnabled = snapshot.AntiLagEnabled == true
    _Yuki_ApplyAntiLag(AntiLagEnabled)

    local rainConfig = _G.PortalVisuals._rainConfig
    if type(rainConfig) == "table" and snapshot.RainRadius then
        rainConfig.RAIN_RADIUS = math.clamp(snapshot.RainRadius, 15, 120)
    end

    local snowConfig = _G.PortalVisuals._snowConfig
    if type(snowConfig) == "table" and snapshot.SnowRadius then
        snowConfig.SpawnRadius = math.clamp(snapshot.SnowRadius, 15, 160)
    end

    for _, effectName in ipairs(PortalVisualEffects) do
        if snapshot.Effects and snapshot.Effects[effectName] then
            _G.PortalVisuals[effectName] = true
        end
    end

    if EffectRangeUI.Rain and snapshot.RainRadius then
        EffectRangeUI.Rain.SetValue(snapshot.RainRadius)
    end
    if EffectRangeUI.Snowfall and snapshot.SnowRadius then
        EffectRangeUI.Snowfall.SetValue(snapshot.SnowRadius)
    end

    CurrentPreset = presetLabel or snapshot.PresetName or "Custom"
    EnginePreset.Text = "Preset: " .. tostring(CurrentPreset)
    EngineStatus.Text = "ACTIVE"

    if RefreshAllSliders then
        RefreshAllSliders()
    end
    if RefreshUIToggles then
        RefreshUIToggles()
    end

    return true
end

function _Yuki_ApplyCustomPresetEntry(entry)
    if not entry or not entry.data then
        return
    end
    _Yuki_ApplyCustomSnapshot(entry.data, entry.name)
end

function _Yuki_CloseCustomNameDialog()
    if CustomNameDialog then
        CustomNameDialog.Visible = false
    end
end

function _Yuki_ShowCustomNameDialog()
    if not CustomNameDialog then
        return
    end
    CustomNameInput.Text = ""
    CustomEditorMessage.Text = ""
    CustomNameDialog.Visible = true
    CustomNameInput:CaptureFocus()
end

function _Yuki_CommitCustomPreset()
    local name = CustomNameInput and CustomNameInput.Text:gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "") or ""
    if name == "" then
        CustomEditorMessage.Text = "h2k4k7k6l0m0k7 l5k2k9k4k2l5l0k7 l7l8k7l9k7m0k2."
        return
    end

    local existingIndex = nil
    for i, entry in ipairs(CustomPresets) do
        if string.lower(entry.name) == string.lower(name) then
            existingIndex = i
            break
        end
    end

    if not existingIndex and #CustomPresets >= 3 then
        CustomEditorMessage.Text = "h4l6l9m0m1l7l5l6 l4k2l2l9l0l4m1l4 3 Custom Presets."
        return
    end

    local entry = {
        name = name,
        data = _Yuki_SnapshotCustomState()
    }

    if existingIndex then
        CustomPresets[existingIndex] = entry
    else
        table.insert(CustomPresets, entry)
    end

    _Yuki_SaveCustomPresetsToFile()
    _Yuki_CloseCustomNameDialog()

    CustomEditorOpen = false
    if CustomEditorOverlay then
        CustomEditorOverlay.Visible = false
    end

    _Yuki_ApplyCustomPresetEntry(entry)
    if CustomEditorOriginal then
        CustomEditorOriginal = nil
    end

    UpdateCustomSection()

    if CustomPlusButton then
        CustomPlusButton.AutoButtonColor = #CustomPresets < 3
        CustomPlusButton.TextColor3 = #CustomPresets < 3 and CONFIG.White or CONFIG.Muted
    end
end

local function CancelCustomEditor()
    if CustomEditorOriginal then
        _Yuki_ApplyCustomSnapshot(CustomEditorOriginal, CustomEditorOriginal.PresetName)
    end
    CustomEditorOriginal = nil
    CustomEditorOpen = false
    _Yuki_CloseCustomNameDialog()
    if CustomEditorOverlay then
        CustomEditorOverlay.Visible = false
    end
end

local function MakeEditorRow(parent, height)
    local row = Instance.new("Frame")
    row.Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, height or 48)
    row.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(250, 247, 248)
    row.BackgroundTransparency = 0
    row.BorderSizePixel = 0
    row.ZIndex = 104
    row.Active = true
    row.Parent = parent
    AddCorner(row, 12)
    AddStroke(row, CONFIG.PurpleLight, 0.70, 1)
    return row
end

local function CreateEditorSlider(parent, definition)
    local row = MakeEditorRow(parent, 58)
    row.Name = "Slider_" .. definition.Key
    local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    label.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(13, 6)
    label.Size = UDim2.new(1, -110, 0, 18)
    label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
    label.Text = definition.Name
    label.TextSize = 10
    label.TextColor3 = CONFIG.Text
    label.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    label.ZIndex = 105
    label.Parent = row

    local valueLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    valueLabel.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0)
    valueLabel.Position = UDim2.new(1, -13, 0, 6)
    valueLabel.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(74, 18)
    valueLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    valueLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold
    valueLabel.TextSize = 9
    valueLabel.TextColor3 = CONFIG.Purple
    valueLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
    valueLabel.ZIndex = 105
    valueLabel.Parent = row

    local track = Instance.new("Frame")
    track.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(13, 36)
    track.Size = UDim2.new(1, -26, 0, 6)
    track.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(232, 228, 230)
    track.BorderSizePixel = 0
    track.ZIndex = 105
    track.Active = true
    track.Parent = row
    AddCorner(track, 10)

    local fill = Instance.new("Frame")
    fill.Size = UDim2.fromScale(0, 1)
    fill.BackgroundColor3 = CONFIG.Purple
    fill.BorderSizePixel = 0
    fill.ZIndex = 106
    fill.Parent = track
    AddCorner(fill, 10)

    local knob = Instance.new("Frame")
    knob.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    knob.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0, 0.5)
    knob.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 12)
    knob.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    knob.BorderSizePixel = 0
    knob.ZIndex = 107
    knob.Active = true
    knob.Parent = track
    AddCorner(knob, 99)

    local dragging = false

    local function getValue()
        return ShaderState[definition.Key]
    end

    row:SetAttribute("SettingKey", definition.Key)

    local function setValue(value, instant)
        value = math.round(value / definition.Step) * definition.Step
        value = Clamp(value, definition.Min, definition.Max)

        ShaderState[definition.Key] = value

        if definition.Key == "Saturation" or definition.Key == "Brightness" or definition.Key == "Contrast" then
            _Yuki_UpdateColorCorrection()
            _Yuki_EnsureEffect("ColorCorrection")
        elseif definition.Key == "Blur" then
            _Yuki_EnsureEffect("Blur")
            _Yuki_UpdateBlur()
        elseif definition.Key == "BloomIntensity" or definition.Key == "BloomSize" then
            _Yuki_EnsureEffect("Bloom")
            _Yuki_UpdateBloom()
        elseif definition.Key == "SunRaysIntensity" or definition.Key == "SunRaysSpread" then
            _Yuki_EnsureEffect("SunRays")
            _Yuki_UpdateSunRays()
        elseif definition.Key == "DOFFarIntensity" or definition.Key == "DOFNearIntensity" or definition.Key == "DOFFocusDistance" then
            _Yuki_EnsureEffect("DepthOfField")
            _Yuki_UpdateDOF()
        elseif definition.Key == "AtmosphereDensity" then
            _Yuki_EnsureEffect("Atmosphere")
            _Yuki_UpdateAtmosphere()
        elseif definition.Key == "MotionBlurStrength" then
            if ShaderState.MotionBlur then
                _Yuki_StartMotionBlur()
            end
        elseif definition.Key == "VignetteStrength" then
            if ShaderState.Vignette then
                _Yuki_UpdateVignette()
            end
        end

        local alpha = (value - definition.Min) / math.max(definition.Max - definition.Min, 0.0001)
        fill.Size = UDim2.fromScale(alpha, 1)
        knob.Position = UDim2.fromScale(alpha, 0.5)
        valueLabel.Text = definition.Step < 1 and string.format("%.2f", value) or tostring(value)
    end

    local function inputToValue(input)
        local width = math.max(track.AbsoluteSize.X, 1)
        local alpha = Clamp((input.Position.X - track.AbsolutePosition.X) / width, 0, 1)
        return definition.Min + (definition.Max - definition.Min) * alpha
    end

    track.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
            or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            dragging = true
            setValue(inputToValue(input))
            PlayUISFX("Click")
        end
    end)
    knob.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
            or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            dragging = true
        end
    end)
    UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if dragging
            and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
                or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
            setValue(inputToValue(input))
        end
    end)
    UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
            or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            dragging = false
        end
    end)

    setValue(getValue() or definition.Default, true)
    CustomEditorRefreshers[definition.Key] = function()
        setValue(ShaderState[definition.Key] or definition.Default, true)
    end
    return row
end

local function CreateEditorToggle(parent, title, getter, setter)
    local row = MakeEditorRow(parent, 44)
    row.Name = "Toggle_" .. title

    local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    label.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(13, 0)
    label.Size = UDim2.new(1, -72, 1, 0)
    label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
    label.Text = title
    label.TextSize = 10
    label.TextColor3 = CONFIG.Text
    label.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    label.ZIndex = 105
    label.Parent = row

    local switch = Instance.new("Frame")
    switch.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5)
    switch.Position = UDim2.new(1, -13, 0.5, 0)
    switch.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(42, 20)
    switch.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(232, 228, 230)
    switch.BorderSizePixel = 0
    switch.ZIndex = 105
    switch.Active = true
    switch.Parent = row
    AddCorner(switch, 99)

    local knob = Instance.new("Frame")
    knob.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(2, 2)
    knob.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 16)
    knob.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    knob.BorderSizePixel = 0
    knob.ZIndex = 106
    knob.Active = true
    knob.Parent = switch
    AddCorner(knob, 99)

    local function refresh()
        local state = getter()
        switch.BackgroundColor3 = state and CONFIG.Purple or Color3.fromRGB(232, 228, 230)
        knob.Position = state and UDim2.fromOffset(24, 2) or UDim2.fromOffset(2, 2)
    end

    row.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
            or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            local state = not getter()
            setter(state)
            refresh()
            PlayUISFX("Click")
        end
    end)

    refresh()
    CustomEditorRefreshers["Toggle:" .. title] = refresh
    return row
end

UpdateCustomSection = function()
    if not CustomSection or not CustomGrid then
        return
    end

    for _, child in ipairs(CustomGrid:GetChildren()) do
        if child:IsA("Frame") then
            child:Destroy()
        end
    end

    for i, entry in ipairs(CustomPresets) do
        local presetIndex = i
        local cell = Instance.new("Frame")
        cell.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        cell.LayoutOrder = i
        cell.ZIndex = 23
        cell.Parent = CustomGrid

        local applyButton = Instance.new("TextButton")
        applyButton.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
        applyButton.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(250, 247, 248)
        applyButton.BackgroundTransparency = 0
        applyButton.BorderSizePixel = 0
        applyButton.AutoButtonColor = false
        applyButton.Text = entry.name
        applyButton.Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold
        applyButton.TextSize = 10
        applyButton.TextColor3 = CONFIG.Text
        applyButton.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
        applyButton.ZIndex = 24
        applyButton.Parent = cell
        AddCorner(applyButton, 13)
        AddStroke(applyButton, CONFIG.PurpleLight, 0.78, 1)

        local x = Instance.new("TextButton")
        x.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0)
        x.Position = UDim2.new(1, -5, 0, 5)
        x.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(22, 22)
        x.BackgroundColor3 = CONFIG.Purple
        x.BackgroundTransparency = 0
        x.BorderSizePixel = 0
        x.AutoButtonColor = false
        x.Text = "×"
        x.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        x.TextSize = 13
        x.TextColor3 = CONFIG.White
        x.ZIndex = 26
        x.Parent = cell
        AddCorner(x, 8)

        applyButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            _Yuki_ApplyCustomPresetEntry(entry)
            Tween(applyButton, {BackgroundColor3 = CONFIG.Purple}, 0.12, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine)
            task.delay(0.15, function()
                if applyButton and applyButton.Parent then
                    Tween(applyButton, {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(250, 247, 248)}, 0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine)
                end
            end)
        end)

        x.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            table.remove(CustomPresets, presetIndex)
            _Yuki_SaveCustomPresetsToFile()
            UpdateCustomSection()
        end)

        BindUISFX(applyButton)
        BindUISFX(x)
    end

    if CustomCountLabel then
        CustomCountLabel.Text = string.format("%d / 3", #CustomPresets)
    end

    if CustomPlusButton then
        local available = #CustomPresets < 3
        CustomPlusButton.Text = "+"
        CustomPlusButton.TextColor3 = available and CONFIG.White or CONFIG.Muted
        CustomPlusButton.BackgroundColor3 = available and CONFIG.Purple or Color3.fromRGB(225, 222, 224)
        CustomPlusButton.Active = available
    end
end

    local function CreateEditorUISlider(parent, key, title, minValue, maxValue, step, getter, setter)
        local row = MakeEditorRow(parent, 58)
        row.Name = "UISlider_" .. key

        local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        label.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(13, 6)
        label.Size = UDim2.new(1, -110, 0, 18)
        label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
        label.Text = title
        label.TextSize = 10
        label.TextColor3 = CONFIG.Text
        label.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        label.ZIndex = 105
        label.Parent = row

        local valueLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        valueLabel.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0)
        valueLabel.Position = UDim2.new(1, -13, 0, 6)
        valueLabel.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(74, 18)
        valueLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        valueLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold
        valueLabel.TextSize = 9
        valueLabel.TextColor3 = CONFIG.Purple
        valueLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
        valueLabel.ZIndex = 105
        valueLabel.Parent = row

        local track = Instance.new("Frame")
        track.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(13, 36)
        track.Size = UDim2.new(1, -26, 0, 6)
        track.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(232, 228, 230)
        track.BorderSizePixel = 0
        track.Active = true
        track.ZIndex = 105
        track.Parent = row
        AddCorner(track, 10)

        local fill = Instance.new("Frame")
        fill.Size = UDim2.fromScale(0, 1)
        fill.BackgroundColor3 = CONFIG.Purple
        fill.BorderSizePixel = 0
        fill.ZIndex = 106
        fill.Parent = track
        AddCorner(fill, 10)

        local knob = Instance.new("Frame")
        knob.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        knob.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0, 0.5)
        knob.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 12)
        knob.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        knob.BorderSizePixel = 0
        knob.Active = true
        knob.ZIndex = 107
        knob.Parent = track
        AddCorner(knob, 99)

        local dragging = false
        local function setValue(value, instant)
            value = math.round(value / step) * step
            value = Clamp(value, minValue, maxValue)
            setter(value)
            local alpha = (value - minValue) / math.max(maxValue - minValue, 0.0001)
            fill.Size = UDim2.fromScale(alpha, 1)
            knob.Position = UDim2.fromScale(alpha, 0.5)
            valueLabel.Text = step < 1 and string.format("%.2f", value) or tostring(value)
        end

        local function inputToValue(input)
            local width = math.max(track.AbsoluteSize.X, 1)
            local alpha = Clamp((input.Position.X - track.AbsolutePosition.X) / width, 0, 1)
            return minValue + (maxValue - minValue) * alpha
        end

        track.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
                or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                dragging = true
                setValue(inputToValue(input))
                PlayUISFX("Click")
            end
        end)
        knob.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
                or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                dragging = true
            end
        end)
        UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
            if dragging and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
                or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
                setValue(inputToValue(input))
            end
        end)
        UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
                or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                dragging = false
            end
        end)

        CustomEditorRefreshers["UI:" .. key] = function()
            setValue(getter(), true)
        end

        setValue(getter(), true)
        return row
    end

    local toggleOrder = 51
    local function addToggle(title, getter, setter)
        local row = CreateEditorToggle(CustomEditorBody, title, getter, setter)
        row.LayoutOrder = toggleOrder
        toggleOrder = toggleOrder + 1
    end

    addToggle("Enable FOV Changer", function() return UISettings.FOVEnabled end, function(v)
        UISettings.FOVEnabled = v
        if Camera then
            Camera.FieldOfView = v and UISettings.FOVValue or 70
        end
    end)

        CustomEditorBody,
        "FOVValue",
        "FOV",
        1,
        120,
        1,

        function(v)
            UISettings.FOVValue = v

                Camera.FieldOfView = v

        end
    )
    fovEditorRow.LayoutOrder = 53

    addToggle("Enable Aspect Ratio", function() return UISettings.AspectEnabled end, function(v)
        UISettings.AspectEnabled = v
    end)

    local aspectEditorRow = CreateEditorUISlider(
        CustomEditorBody,
        "AspectValue",
        "Ratio Value",
        0.05,
        1.14,
        0.01,
        function() return UISettings.AspectValue end,
        function(v)
            UISettings.AspectValue = v
        end
    )
    aspectEditorRow.LayoutOrder = 55

    addToggle("Motion Blur", function() return ShaderState.MotionBlur end, function(v)
        ShaderState.MotionBlur = v
        if v then
            ShaderState.MotionBlurStrength = math.max(ShaderState.MotionBlurStrength, 0.15)
            _Yuki_StartMotionBlur()
        else
            _Yuki_StopMotionBlur()
        end
    end)

    addToggle("Vignette", function() return ShaderState.Vignette end, function(v)
        ShaderState.Vignette = v
        if v then
            ShaderState.VignetteStrength = math.max(ShaderState.VignetteStrength, 0.30)
            _Yuki_UpdateVignette()
        else
            _Yuki_DestroyVignette()
        end
    end)

    addToggle("Kill Atmosphere", function() return UISettings.KillAtmosphere end, function(v)
        UISettings.KillAtmosphere = v
        _Yuki_ApplyKillAtmosphere(v)
    end)

    addToggle("Anti Lag", function() return AntiLagEnabled end, function(v)
        AntiLagEnabled = v
        _Yuki_ApplyAntiLag(v)
    end)

    local rangeSection = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    rangeSection.Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 23)
    rangeSection.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    rangeSection.Text = "Effect ranges"
    rangeSection.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    rangeSection.TextSize = 11
    rangeSection.TextColor3 = CONFIG.Purple
    rangeSection.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    rangeSection.LayoutOrder = 60
    rangeSection.ZIndex = 104
    rangeSection.Parent = CustomEditorBody

    local rainRangeRow = CreateEditorUISlider(
        CustomEditorBody,
        "RainRadius",
        "Rain Range",
        15,
        120,
        1,
        function()
            local cfg = _G.PortalVisuals._rainConfig
            return (type(cfg) == "table" and cfg.RAIN_RADIUS) or 45
        end,
        function(v)
            local cfg = _G.PortalVisuals._rainConfig
            if type(cfg) == "table" then
                cfg.RAIN_RADIUS = v
            end
            if _G.PortalVisuals._rainSync then
                _G.PortalVisuals._rainSync()
            end
        end
    )
    rainRangeRow.LayoutOrder = 61

    local snowRangeRow = CreateEditorUISlider(
        CustomEditorBody,
        "SnowRadius",
        "Snow Range",
        15,
        160,
        1,
        function()
            local cfg = _G.PortalVisuals._snowConfig

            if type(cfg) == "table" then
                cfg.SpawnRadius = v

    snowRangeRow.LayoutOrder = 62

    local effectsSection = Instance.new("TextLabel")

    effectsSection.Text = "Visual Effects"
    effectsSection.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    effectsSection.TextSize = 11
    effectsSection.TextColor3 = CONFIG.Purple
    effectsSection.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    effectsSection.LayoutOrder = 70
    effectsSection.ZIndex = 104
    effectsSection.Parent = CustomEditorBody

    local effectOrder = 71
    for _, effectName in ipairs(PortalVisualEffects) do

    end

    -- Action buttons.
    local cancel = Instance.new("TextButton")
    cancel.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(180, 38)
    cancel.Position = UDim2.new(1, -385, 1, -50)
    cancel.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(238, 235, 237)
    cancel.BorderSizePixel = 0
    cancel.AutoButtonColor = false
    cancel.Text = "i4m0l4k7l5l0m0n0"
    cancel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold
    cancel.TextSize = 11
    cancel.TextColor3 = CONFIG.Text
    cancel.ZIndex = 106
    cancel.Parent = CustomEditorFrame
    AddCorner(cancel, 11)
    AddStroke(cancel, CONFIG.PurpleLight, 0.68, 1)

    local save = Instance.new("TextButton")

    cancelName.MouseButton1Click:Connect(_Yuki_CloseCustomNameDialog)

    CustomNameInput.FocusLost:Connect(function(enterPressed)
        if enterPressed and CustomNameDialog.Visible then
            _Yuki_CommitCustomPreset()
        end
    end)

    BindUISFX(cancelName)
    BindUISFX(saveName)

    local function refreshCanvas()
        CustomEditorBody.CanvasSize = UDim2.fromOffset(
            0,
            bodyLayout.AbsoluteContentSize.Y + 8
        )
    end
    bodyLayout:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteContentSize"):Connect(refreshCanvas)
    refreshCanvas()

    local function resetEditorUIValues()
        for _, child in ipairs(CustomEditorBody:GetChildren()) do
            if child:IsA("Frame") and child.Name ~= "" then
                -- sliders refresh themselves from current state only when rebuilt;
                -- opening always uses the live current state, so no forced mutation here.
            end
        end
    end

    local _ = resetEditorUIValues
end

OpenCustomEditor = function()
    if CustomEditorOpen or #CustomPresets >= 3 then
        return
    end

    CustomEditorOriginal = _Yuki_SnapshotCustomState()
    CustomEditorOpen = true

    if CustomEditorOverlay then
        CustomEditorOverlay.Visible = true
    end
    if CustomNameDialog then
        CustomNameDialog.Visible = false
    end
    if CustomEditorBody then
        CustomEditorBody.CanvasPosition = Vector2.zero
    end

    for _, definition in ipairs(SettingDefinitions) do
        local refresher = CustomEditorRefreshers[definition.Key]
        if refresher then
            refresher()
        end
    end
    for key, refresher in pairs(CustomEditorRefreshers) do
        if string.sub(key, 1, 7) == "Toggle:" or string.sub(key, 1, 3) == "UI:" then
            refresher()
        end
    end

    EngineStatus.Text = "EDITING"
end

_Yuki_LoadCustomPresetsFromFile()
BuildCustomPresetUI()

-- Rebuild custom list now that the UI exists.
UpdateCustomSection()

--============================================================
-- RESET SETTINGS
--============================================================

Connect(
    ResetSettingsButton.MouseButton1Click,
    function()

        _Yuki_ApplyPreset(
            "Default"
        )

        _Yuki_ApplyAntiLag(false)

        RefreshAllSliders()
        RefreshUIToggles()

        EnginePreset.Text =
            "Preset: Default"
    end
)

local Navigation =
    Instance.new("Frame")

Navigation.Name =
    "Navigation"

Navigation.Position =
    UDim2.new(
        0,
        18,
        1,
        -84
    )

Navigation.Size =
    UDim2.new(
        1,
        -36,
        0,
        62
    )

    1

    0

Navigation.ZIndex =
    50

    Content

local ButtonWidth = 132
local ButtonHeight = 56
local ActionSize = 56
local ButtonGap = 6

local function BuildHomeIcon(parent)

    -- Body: solid white
    local body =
        Instance.new("Frame")

    body.Position =
        UDim2.fromOffset(8, 14)

    body.Size =
        UDim2.fromOffset(20, 15)

    body.BackgroundColor3 =
        CONFIG.White

    body.BorderSizePixel = 0
    body.ZIndex = 55
    body.Parent = parent

    AddCorner(body, 3)

    -- Roof: two clean white strokes meeting at the center
    local roofLeft =
        Instance.new("Frame")

    roofLeft.AnchorPoint =
        Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)

    roofLeft.Position =
        UDim2.fromOffset(13, 10)

    roofLeft.Size =
        UDim2.fromOffset(15, 4)

    roofLeft.BackgroundColor3 =
        CONFIG.White

    roofLeft.BorderSizePixel = 0
    roofLeft.Rotation = 31
    roofLeft.ZIndex = 56
    roofLeft.Parent = parent

    AddCorner(roofLeft, 3)

    local roofRight = roofLeft:Clone()

    roofRight.Position =
        UDim2.fromOffset(23, 10)

    roofRight.Rotation = -31
    roofRight.Parent = parent

    -- Door: also pure white; no purple cut-out
    local door =
        Instance.new("Frame")

    door.AnchorPoint =
        Vector2.new(0.5, 1)

    door.Position =
        UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 1)

    door.Size =
        UDim2.fromOffset(6, 9)

    door.BackgroundColor3 =
        CONFIG.White

    door.BorderSizePixel = 0
    door.ZIndex = 57
    door.Parent = body

    AddCorner(door, 2)
end

local function BuildGridIcon(parent)

    for x = 0, 1 do
        for y = 0, 1 do

            local cube =
                Instance.new("Frame")

            cube.Position =
                UDim2.fromOffset(7 + x * 12, 7 + y * 12)

            cube.Size =
                UDim2.fromOffset(9, 9)

            cube.BackgroundColor3 =
                CONFIG.White

            cube.BorderSizePixel = 0
            cube.ZIndex = 55
            cube.Parent = parent

            AddCorner(cube, 3)
        end
    end
end

local function BuildSettingsIcon(parent)

    -- Pure white cog. The center is also white so the whole icon is one color.
    local center =
        Instance.new("Frame")

    center.AnchorPoint =
        Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)

    center.Position =
        UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)

    center.Size =
        UDim2.fromOffset(13, 13)

    center.BackgroundColor3 =
        CONFIG.White

    center.BorderSizePixel = 0
    center.ZIndex = 56
    center.Parent = parent

    AddCorner(center, 99)

    for i = 0, 7 do

        local tooth =
            Instance.new("Frame")

        tooth.AnchorPoint =
            Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)

        tooth.Position =
            UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)

        tooth.Size =
            UDim2.fromOffset(5, 10)

        tooth.BackgroundColor3 =
            CONFIG.White

        tooth.BorderSizePixel = 0
        tooth.Rotation = i * 45
        tooth.ZIndex = 55
        tooth.Parent = parent

        AddCorner(tooth, 2)
    end
end

    knob.ZIndex = 56

end

local NavButtons = {}

local function CreateNavButton(
    index,
    name,
    description,
    iconBuilder
)

    local button =
        Instance.new("TextButton")

    button.Name =
        name

    local xOffset = 0
    if index > 1 then
        for i = 1, index - 1 do
            local previousWidth = (i >= 5) and ActionSize or ButtonWidth
            xOffset = xOffset + previousWidth + ButtonGap
        end
    end

    button.Position =
        UDim2.fromOffset(
            xOffset,
            0
        )

    button.Size =
        UDim2.fromOffset(
            ButtonWidth,
            ButtonHeight
        )

    button.BackgroundColor3 =
        CONFIG.PurpleSoft

    button.BackgroundTransparency =
        0.82

    button.BorderSizePixel =
        0

    button.AutoButtonColor =
        false

    button.Text =
        ""

    button.ZIndex =
        51

    button.Parent =
        Navigation

    AddCorner(
        button,
        CONFIG.ButtonRadius
    )

    local stroke =
        AddStroke(
            button,
            CONFIG.PurpleLight,
            0.68,
            1.1
        )

    local iconHolder =
        Instance.new("Frame")

    iconHolder.Position =
        UDim2.fromOffset(
            7,
            7
        )

    iconHolder.Size =
        UDim2.fromOffset(
            42,
            42
        )

    iconHolder.BackgroundColor3 =
        CONFIG.Purple

    iconHolder.BackgroundTransparency =
        0.52

    iconHolder.BorderSizePixel =
        0

    iconHolder.ZIndex =
        52

    iconHolder.Parent =
        button

    AddCorner(
        iconHolder,
        11
    )

    iconBuilder(
        iconHolder
    )

    local title =
        Instance.new("TextLabel")

    title.Position =
        UDim2.fromOffset(
            60,
            8
        )

    title.Size =
        UDim2.new(
            1,
            -60,
            0,
            18
        )

    title.BackgroundTransparency =
        1

    title.Font =
        Enum.Font.GothamSemibold

    title.Text =
        name

    title.TextSize =
        11

    title.TextColor3 =
        CONFIG.Text

    title.TextXAlignment =
        Enum.TextXAlignment.Left

    title.ZIndex =
        53

    title.Parent =
        button

    local sub =
        Instance.new("TextLabel")

    sub.Position =
        UDim2.fromOffset(
            60,
            29
        )

    sub.Size =
        UDim2.new(
            1,
            -60,
            0,
            15
        )

    sub.BackgroundTransparency =
        1

    sub.Font =
        Enum.Font.Gotham

    sub.Text =
        description

    sub.TextSize =
        8

    sub.TextColor3 =
        CONFIG.Muted

    sub.TextXAlignment =
        Enum.TextXAlignment.Left

    sub.ZIndex =
        53

    sub.Parent =
        button

    -- Collapse and Exit are deliberately compact square action buttons.
    if name == "Collapse" or name == "Exit" then
        button.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(ActionSize, ButtonHeight)
        iconHolder.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(7, 7)
        iconHolder.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(42, 42)
        title.Visible = false
        sub.Visible = false
        AddCorner(button, 12)
    end

    Connect(
        button.MouseEnter,
        function()

            Tween(
                button,
                {
                    BackgroundColor3 =
                        CONFIG.Purple,

                    BackgroundTransparency =
                        0.36,

                    Rotation =
                        0.45
                },
                0.22,
                Enum.EasingStyle.Sine
            )

            Tween(
                stroke,
                {
                    Transparency =
                        0.45,

                    Thickness =
                        1.3
                },
                0.22,
                Enum.EasingStyle.Sine
            )

            Tween(
                iconHolder,
                {
                    BackgroundTransparency =
                        0,

                    Size =
                        UDim2.fromOffset(
                            38,
                            38
                        ),

                    Position =
                        UDim2.fromOffset(
                            7,
                            6
                        )
                },
                0.22,
                Enum.EasingStyle.Back
            )

            Tween(
                title,
                {
                    TextColor3 =
                        CONFIG.White
                },
                0.18
            )
        end
    )

    -- Small spring-like press animation. This stays scoped inside the button
    -- factory, so it does not increase the script chunk local-register count.
    local pressScale = Instance.new("UIScale")
    pressScale.Scale = 1
    pressScale.Parent = button

    Connect(
        button.MouseButton1Click,
        function()
            Tween(pressScale, {Scale = 0.94}, 0.06, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
            task.delay(0.07, function()
                if button and button.Parent then
                    Tween(pressScale, {Scale = 1.035}, 0.10, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
                    task.delay(0.10, function()
                        if button and button.Parent then
                            Tween(pressScale, {Scale = 1}, 0.12, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end)
        end
    )

    Connect(
        button.MouseLeave,
        function()

            Tween(
                button,
                {
                    BackgroundColor3 =
                        CONFIG.PurpleSoft,

                    BackgroundTransparency =
                        0.82,

                    Rotation =
                        0
                },
                0.30,
                Enum.EasingStyle.Sine
            )

            Tween(
                stroke,
                {
                    Transparency =
                        0.87,

                    Thickness =
                        1
                },
                0.30,
                Enum.EasingStyle.Sine
            )

            Tween(
                iconHolder,
                {
                    BackgroundTransparency =
                        0,

                    Size =
                        UDim2.fromOffset(
                            36,
                            36
                        ),

                    Position =
                        UDim2.fromOffset(
                            8,
                            7
                        )
                },
                0.30,
                Enum.EasingStyle.Sine
            )

            Tween(
                title,
                {
                    TextColor3 =
                        CONFIG.Text
                },
                0.22
            )
        end
    )

    NavButtons[name] =
        button

    return button
end

local HomeButton =
    CreateNavButton(
        1,
        "Home",
        "About project",
        BuildHomeIcon
    )

--============================================================
-- PAGE SWITCHING
--============================================================

local Pages = {
    Home = HomePage,
    Presets = PresetsPage,
    Settings = SettingsPage,
    Effects = EffectsPage
}

local function SwitchPage(
    pageName
)
    local page =
        Pages[pageName]

    if not page then
        return
    end

    if CurrentPage ==
        pageName then
        return
    end

    CurrentPage =
        pageName

    for _, pageObject in pairs(
        Pages
    ) do

        pageObject.Visible =
            false
    end

    page.Visible =
        true

    page.Position =
        UDim2.fromOffset(
            8,
            0
        )

    Tween(
        page,
        {
            Position =
                UDim2.fromOffset(
                    0,
                    0
                )
        },
        0.32,
        Enum.EasingStyle.Quint
    )
end

Connect(
    HomeButton.MouseButton1Click,
    function()
        SwitchPage(
            "Home"
        )
    end
)

Connect(
    PresetsButton.MouseButton1Click,
    function()
        SwitchPage(
            "Presets"
        )
    end
)

Connect(
    SettingsButton.MouseButton1Click,
    function()
        SwitchPage(
            "Settings"
        )
    end
)

Connect(
    EffectsButton.MouseButton1Click,
    function()
        SwitchPage(
            "Effects"
        )
    end
)

--============================================================
-- COLLAPSE
--============================================================

local function SetCollapsed(
    state
)
    if not Alive then
        return
    end

    if Collapsed ==
        state then
        return
    end

    Collapsed =
        state

    if state then
        SetMenuBlur(false)

        SavedPosition =
            Main.Position

        Tween(
            Main,
            {
                Size =
                    UDim2.fromOffset(
                        8,
                        8
                    ),

                BackgroundTransparency =
                    0
            },
            0.42,
            Enum.EasingStyle.Quint,
            Enum.EasingDirection.In
        )

        task.delay(
            0.28,
            function()

                if not Alive
                    or not Collapsed then
                    return
                end

                Main.Visible =
                    false
            end
        )

    else

        Main.Visible =
            true
        SetMenuBlur(true)

        Main.Position =
            SavedPosition

        Main.Size =
            UDim2.fromOffset(
                8,
                8
            )

        Main.BackgroundTransparency =
            0

        Tween(
            Main,
            {
                Size =
                    UDim2.fromOffset(
                        CONFIG.Width,
                        CONFIG.Height
                    ),

                BackgroundTransparency =
                    0
            },
            0.48,
            Enum.EasingStyle.Quint,
            Enum.EasingDirection.Out
        )
    end
end

Connect(
    CollapseButton.MouseButton1Click,
    function()
        SetCollapsed(
            true
        )
    end
)

Connect(
    UserInputService.InputBegan,
    function(
        input,
        processed
    )

        if processed then
            return
        end

        if input.KeyCode ==
            CONFIG.RightAlt then

            if ConfirmOpen then
                return
            end

            SetCollapsed(
                not Collapsed
            )
        end
    end
)

local function OpenConfirmation()

    ConfirmOpen =
        true

    Overlay.Visible =
        true

    Overlay.BackgroundTransparency =
        1

    Confirm.Size =
        UDim2.fromOffset(
            375,
            195
        )

    Tween(
        Overlay,
        {
            BackgroundTransparency =
                0.28
        },
        0.23,
        Enum.EasingStyle.Sine
    )

    Tween(
        Confirm,
        {
            Size =
                UDim2.fromOffset(
                    420,
                    220
                )
        },
        0.38,
        Enum.EasingStyle.Back
    )
end

local function CloseConfirmation()

    Tween(
        Overlay,
        {
            BackgroundTransparency =
                1
        },
        0.18,
        Enum.EasingStyle.Sine
    )

    Tween(
        Confirm,
        {
            Size =
                UDim2.fromOffset(
                    375,
                    195
                )
        },
        0.18,
        Enum.EasingStyle.Quint
    )

    task.delay(
        0.19,
        function()

            Overlay.Visible =
                false

            ConfirmOpen =
                false
        end
    )
end

Connect(
    ExitButton.MouseButton1Click,
    OpenConfirmation
)

Connect(
    NoButton.MouseButton1Click,
    CloseConfirmation
)

--============================================================
-- UNLOAD
--============================================================

local function Unload()

    if not Alive then
        return
    end

    Alive =
        false

    SetMenuBlur(false)
    DisconnectAll()

    -- Never leave preset-modified Lighting / Fog / Sky / Clouds behind
    -- after the engine is unloaded.
    _Yuki_RestoreKilledAtmosphere()
    _Yuki_RestoreOriginalWorld()

    _Yuki_DestroyShaderEffects()
    _Yuki_DestroyVignette()
    _Yuki_StopMotionBlur()

    if Camera then
        pcall(function()
            Camera.FieldOfView = 70
        end)
    end

    Tween(
        Main,
        {
            Size =
                UDim2.fromOffset(
                    5,
                    5
                ),

            BackgroundTransparency =
                1
        },
        0.30,
        Enum.EasingStyle.Quint,
        Enum.EasingDirection.In
    )

    task.delay(
        0.32,
        function()
            SafeDestroy(
                ScreenGui
            )
        end
    )
end

Connect(
    YesButton.MouseButton1Click,
    Unload
)

--============================================================
-- DRAG
--============================================================

Connect(
    Header.InputBegan,
    function(input)

        if input.UserInputType ==
            Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then

            Dragging =
                true

            DragStart =
                input.Position

            DragOrigin =
                Main.Position
        end
    end
)

Connect(
    UserInputService.InputEnded,
    function(input)

        if input.UserInputType ==
            Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then

            Dragging =
                false
        end
    end
)

Connect(
    UserInputService.InputChanged,
    function(input)

        if not Dragging then
            return
        end

        if input.UserInputType ~=
            Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement then

            return
        end

        local delta =
            input.Position
            -
            DragStart

        Main.Position =
            UDim2.new(
                DragOrigin.X.Scale,
                DragOrigin.X.Offset
                    + delta.X,

                DragOrigin.Y.Scale,
                DragOrigin.Y.Offset
                    + delta.Y
            )

        SavedPosition =
            Main.Position
    end
)

--============================================================
-- BUBBLES
--============================================================

local RNG =
    Random.new()

local function CreateBubble(
    index
)

    local size =
        RNG:NextNumber(10, 26)

    local bubble =
        Instance.new("Frame")

    bubble.Name =
        "Bubble_" .. index

    bubble.AnchorPoint =
        Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)

    bubble.Size =
        UDim2.fromOffset(size, size)

    bubble.BackgroundColor3 =
        CONFIG.Purple

    bubble.BackgroundTransparency =
        0.72

    bubble.BorderSizePixel = 0
    bubble.ZIndex = 6
    bubble.Parent = BubbleLayer

    AddCorner(bubble, 99)

    local stroke =
        AddStroke(
            bubble,
            CONFIG.PurpleLight,
            0.58,
            0.8
        )

    local shine =
        Instance.new("Frame")

    shine.AnchorPoint =
        Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)

    shine.Position =
        UDim2.fromScale(0.29, 0.27)

    shine.Size =
        UDim2.fromScale(0.23, 0.23)

    shine.BackgroundColor3 =
        CONFIG.White

    shine.BackgroundTransparency =
        0.35

    shine.BorderSizePixel = 0
    shine.ZIndex = 7
    shine.Parent = bubble

    AddCorner(shine, 99)

    local startX =
        RNG:NextNumber(20, CONFIG.Width - 20)

    local startY =
        RNG:NextNumber(30, CONFIG.Height - 90)

    bubble.Position =
        UDim2.fromOffset(startX, startY)

    table.insert(
        Bubbles,
        {
            Object = bubble,
            Stroke = stroke,

            X = startX,
            Y = startY,

            VX = RNG:NextNumber(-18, 18),
            VY = RNG:NextNumber(-12, 14),

            Phase = RNG:NextNumber(0, math.pi * 2),

            Radius = size,

            RingRadius = RNG:NextNumber(32, 125),
            RingSpeed = RNG:NextNumber(0.18, 0.55),
            RingWobble = RNG:NextNumber(4, 14)
        }
    )
end

for i = 1, CONFIG.BubbleCount do
    CreateBubble(i)
end


--============================================================
-- GLASS SURFACE PASS
--============================================================

local function ApplyGlassSurface()
    if not Main then return end
    for _, obj in ipairs(Main:GetDescendants()) do
        if obj:IsA("TextButton") then
            if obj ~= ExitButton and obj ~= CollapseButton then
                obj.BackgroundTransparency = math.max(obj.BackgroundTransparency, 0.48)
            else
                obj.BackgroundTransparency = math.max(obj.BackgroundTransparency, 0.42)
            end
        elseif obj:IsA("Frame") then
            if obj ~= OuterGlow and obj ~= BubbleLayer and obj ~= Content and obj ~= Header then
                local n = obj.Name
                if n ~= "IconHolder" and n ~= "StatusDot" then
                    obj.BackgroundTransparency = math.max(obj.BackgroundTransparency, 0.48)
                end
            end
        end
    end
end

ApplyGlassSurface()

--============================================================
-- BUBBLE PHYSICS
--============================================================

Connect(
    RunService.RenderStepped,
    function(deltaTime)

        if not Alive then
            return
        end

        if not Main.Visible then
            return
        end

        local mouse =
            UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()

        local mainPosition =
            Main.AbsolutePosition

        local mainSize =
            Main.AbsoluteSize

        local mouseX =
            mouse.X - mainPosition.X

        local mouseY =
            mouse.Y - mainPosition.Y

        local inside =
            mouseX >= 0
            and mouseX <= mainSize.X
            and mouseY >= 0
            and mouseY <= mainSize.Y

        local width =
            math.max(mainSize.X, 1)

        local height =
            math.max(mainSize.Y, 1)

        if inside then

            local time = os.clock()

            -- Every bubble gets its own angle and radius.
            -- This creates a proper halo around the cursor instead of one blob.
            for _, bubble in ipairs(Bubbles) do

                local angle =
                    bubble.Phase
                    + time * bubble.RingSpeed

                local wobble =
                    math.sin(
                        time * 1.15
                        + bubble.Phase * 2
                    )
                    * bubble.RingWobble

                local ring =
                    bubble.RingRadius + wobble

                local targetX =
                    mouseX
                    + math.cos(angle) * ring

                local targetY =
                    mouseY
                    + math.sin(angle) * ring

                targetX =
                    Clamp(
                        targetX,
                        bubble.Radius + 5,
                        width - bubble.Radius - 5
                    )

                targetY =
                    Clamp(
                        targetY,
                        bubble.Radius + 5,
                        height - bubble.Radius - 5
                    )

                local dx =
                    targetX - bubble.X

                local dy =
                    targetY - bubble.Y

                -- Spring toward the bubble's own point on the ring.
                bubble.VX =
                    bubble.VX
                    + dx * CONFIG.BubbleMagnet * 0.92 * deltaTime

                bubble.VY =
                    bubble.VY
                    + dy * CONFIG.BubbleMagnet * 0.92 * deltaTime

                -- Tangential movement makes the halo feel alive.
                bubble.VX =
                    bubble.VX
                    - math.sin(angle) * 7 * deltaTime

                bubble.VY =
                    bubble.VY
                    + math.cos(angle) * 7 * deltaTime

                local drag =
                    math.pow(0.86, deltaTime * 60)

                bubble.VX =
                    bubble.VX * drag

                bubble.VY =
                    bubble.VY * drag

                bubble.X =
                    bubble.X + bubble.VX * deltaTime

                bubble.Y =
                    bubble.Y + bubble.VY * deltaTime

                bubble.Object.Position =
                    UDim2.fromOffset(
                        bubble.X,
                        bubble.Y
                    )
            end

        else

            for _, bubble in ipairs(Bubbles) do

                bubble.VY =
                    bubble.VY
                    + CONFIG.BubbleGravity * deltaTime

                bubble.VX =
                    bubble.VX
                    * math.pow(0.992, deltaTime * 60)

                bubble.VX =
                    bubble.VX
                    + math.sin(
                        os.clock() * 0.7
                        + bubble.Phase
                    ) * 0.4

                bubble.X =
                    bubble.X + bubble.VX * deltaTime

                bubble.Y =
                    bubble.Y + bubble.VY * deltaTime

                local floor =
                    height - bubble.Radius - 7

                if bubble.Y >= floor then

                    bubble.Y = floor
                    bubble.VY = -math.abs(bubble.VY) * 0.22

                    if math.abs(bubble.VY) < 7 then
                        bubble.VY = 0
                    end
                end

                if bubble.X < -bubble.Radius then

                    bubble.X = width + bubble.Radius
                    bubble.Y = RNG:NextNumber(
                        height * 0.22,
                        height * 0.55
                    )
                    bubble.VY = RNG:NextNumber(0, 15)
                end

                if bubble.X > width + bubble.Radius then

                    bubble.X = -bubble.Radius
                    bubble.Y = RNG:NextNumber(
                        height * 0.22,
                        height * 0.55
                    )
                    bubble.VY = RNG:NextNumber(0, 15)
                end

                bubble.Object.Position =
                    UDim2.fromOffset(
                        bubble.X,
                        bubble.Y
                    )
            end
        end
    end
)

--============================================================
-- BUBBLE VISUAL ANIMATION
--============================================================

task.spawn(function()

    while Alive do

        for _, bubble in ipairs(
            Bubbles
        ) do

            if bubble.Object
                and bubble.Object.Parent then

                Tween(
                    bubble.Object,
                    {
                        BackgroundTransparency =
                            0
                    },
                    RNG:NextNumber(
                        0.9,
                        1.6
                    ),
                    Enum.EasingStyle.Sine,
                    Enum.EasingDirection.InOut
                )

                Tween(
                    bubble.Stroke,
                    {
                        Transparency =
                            0
                    },
                    RNG:NextNumber(
                        0.9,
                        1.6
                    ),
                    Enum.EasingStyle.Sine,
                    Enum.EasingDirection.InOut
                )
            end
        end

        task.wait(
            0.55
        )
    end
end)

    Header.InputBegan,
    function(input)

        if input.UserInputType ==
            Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then

            Dragging =
                true

            DragStart =
                input.Position

            DragOrigin =
                Main.Position
        end
    end
)

--============================================================
-- KEY SYSTEM (k6l6k3k2k4l3k7l5)
--============================================================

local correctKey = "YukiShaderUpdate001"
local botLink = "https://t.me/yukishaderenginekey_bot"
local keyFileName = "YukiShaderKey.txt"

local function hasKey()
    local success, result = pcall(function()
        if writefile and readfile then
            local content = readfile(keyFileName)
            if content == correctKey then
                return true
            end
        end
    end)
    return success and result or false
end

local function showMain()
    Main.Visible = true
    Main.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(20, 20)
    Main.BackgroundTransparency = 0
    Tween(
        Main,
        {
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(CONFIG.Width, CONFIG.Height),
            BackgroundTransparency = 0
        },
        0.62,
        Enum.EasingStyle.Quint,
        Enum.EasingDirection.Out
    )
end

if hasKey() then
    showMain()
    SetMenuBlur(true)
else
    -- i7l6k9k6k2n5l4 l6l2l5l6 k4k4l6k6k2 l2l3n2m5k2
    local keyGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    keyGui.Name = "YukiKeySystem"
    keyGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
    keyGui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
    keyGui.DisplayOrder = 1000000
    keyGui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
    pcall(function()
        if syn and syn.protect_gui then
            syn.protect_gui(keyGui)
        end
    end)
    keyGui.Parent = CoreGui

    local keyFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
    keyFrame.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    keyFrame.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
    keyFrame.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(420, 250)
    keyFrame.BackgroundColor3 = CONFIG.BackgroundTop
    keyFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 0.02
    keyFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
    keyFrame.ClipsDescendants = true
    keyFrame.ZIndex = 1
    keyFrame.Parent = keyGui
    AddCorner(keyFrame, CONFIG.WindowRadius)
    AddStroke(keyFrame, CONFIG.PurpleLight, 0.56, 1.25)
    AddGradient(keyFrame, CONFIG.BackgroundTop, CONFIG.BackgroundBottom, 35)

    local keyTitle = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    keyTitle.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(20, 15)
    keyTitle.Size = UDim2.new(1, -40, 0, 55)
    keyTitle.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    keyTitle.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    keyTitle.Text = "Hello! You are in the BEST shaders script for roblox.\nHow to get key? Click on the \"Get Key\" button and go to my telegram bot.\nSubscribe to my channel then copy the key and paste! This is easy!"
    keyTitle.TextSize = 11
    keyTitle.TextColor3 = CONFIG.Text
    keyTitle.TextWrapped = true
    keyTitle.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    keyTitle.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
    keyTitle.ZIndex = 10
    keyTitle.Parent = keyFrame

    local keyInput = Instance.new("TextBox")
    keyInput.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(20, 80)
    keyInput.Size = UDim2.new(1, -40, 0, 35)
    keyInput.BackgroundColor3 = CONFIG.PanelLight
    keyInput.BackgroundTransparency = 0.15
    keyInput.BorderSizePixel = 0
    keyInput.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
    keyInput.Text = ""
    keyInput.PlaceholderText = "Enter key here..."
    keyInput.TextSize = 13
    keyInput.TextColor3 = CONFIG.Text
    keyInput.PlaceholderColor3 = CONFIG.Muted
    keyInput.ZIndex = 10
    keyInput.Parent = keyFrame
    AddCorner(keyInput, 10)
    AddStroke(keyInput, CONFIG.PurpleLight, 0.8, 1)

    local keyMessage = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    keyMessage.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(20, 120)
    keyMessage.Size = UDim2.new(1, -40, 0, 20)
    keyMessage.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    keyMessage.Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold
    keyMessage.Text = ""
    keyMessage.TextSize = 10
    keyMessage.TextColor3 = CONFIG.Text
    keyMessage.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    keyMessage.ZIndex = 10
    keyMessage.Parent = keyFrame

    local getKeyButton = Instance.new("TextButton")
    getKeyButton.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(20, 150)
    getKeyButton.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(180, 35)
    getKeyButton.BackgroundColor3 = CONFIG.Purple
    getKeyButton.BackgroundTransparency = 0.05
    getKeyButton.BorderSizePixel = 0
    getKeyButton.AutoButtonColor = false
    getKeyButton.Text = "Get Key"
    getKeyButton.Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold
    getKeyButton.TextSize = 12
    getKeyButton.TextColor3 = CONFIG.White
    getKeyButton.ZIndex = 10
    getKeyButton.Parent = keyFrame
    AddCorner(getKeyButton, 10)
    AddStroke(getKeyButton, CONFIG.PurpleLight, 0.45, 1)

    local executeButton = Instance.new("TextButton")
    executeButton.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(220, 150)
    executeButton.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(180, 35)
    executeButton.BackgroundColor3 = CONFIG.PurpleSoft
    executeButton.BackgroundTransparency = 0.05
    executeButton.BorderSizePixel = 0
    executeButton.AutoButtonColor = false
    executeButton.Text = "Execute!"
    executeButton.Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold
    executeButton.TextSize = 12
    executeButton.TextColor3 = CONFIG.White
    executeButton.ZIndex = 10
    executeButton.Parent = keyFrame

    BindUISFX(getKeyButton)
    BindUISFX(executeButton)
    AddCorner(executeButton, 10)
    AddStroke(executeButton, CONFIG.PurpleLight, 0.45, 1)

    -- i0l5l6l7l2k2 Get Key
    Connect(getKeyButton.MouseButton1Click, function()
        pcall(function()
            if setclipboard then
                setclipboard(botLink)
            elseif Clipboard then
                Clipboard:Set(botLink)
            end
        end)
        keyInput.Text = ""
        keyMessage.Text = "Bot link copied!"
        keyMessage.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 0)
    end)

    -- i0l5l6l7l2k2 Execute
    Connect(executeButton.MouseButton1Click, function()
        local input = keyInput.Text
        if input == correctKey then
            keyMessage.Text = "YaY! Correct key! Execute script."
            keyMessage.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 0)
            task.delay(0.5, function()
                pcall(function()
                    if writefile then
                        writefile(keyFileName, correctKey)
                    end
                end)
                showMain()
                pcall(function()
                    keyGui:Destroy()
                end)
            end)
        else
            keyMessage.Text = "Incorrect key!"
            keyMessage.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)
        end
    end)
end

--============================================================
-- TEXT STYLE
--============================================================
-- Plain black text, with the outline fully disabled.
local function ImproveTextReadability(root)
    for _, object in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
        if object:IsA("TextLabel") or object:IsA("TextButton") or object:IsA("TextBox") then
            pcall(function()
                object.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
                object.TextStrokeTransparency = 1
            end)
        end
    end
end

ImproveTextReadability(ScreenGui)

-- Bind sounds to all menu buttons that already exist.
for _, descendant in ipairs(ScreenGui:GetDescendants()) do
    if descendant:IsA("TextButton") then
        BindUISFX(descendant)
    end
end
